## 1. Greetings and Polite Expressions (Salutations et politesse)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le bonjour** | Hello / Good morning | `luh bohn-ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20bonjour&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Bonjour madame, comment allez-vous ? *(Good morning ma'am, how are you?)* | les bonjours | Pronounced [bɔ̃ʒuʁ]. No liaison. 'Bonjour' is used all day until evening. |
| **le bonsoir** | Good evening | `luh bohn-SWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20bonsoir&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Bonsoir à tous et bienvenue. *(Good evening everyone and welcome.)* | les bonsoirs | Used from ~18:00 onwards as greeting and farewell. |
| **le salut** | Hi / Bye (informal) | `luh sah-LYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20salut&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Salut Thomas, tu viens avec nous ? *(Hi Thomas, are you coming with us?)* | les saluts | Silent final 't' [saly]. Strictly for friends and family. |
| **au revoir** | Goodbye | `oh ruh-VWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=au%20revoir&op=translate) | `Expression` | Merci beaucoup et au revoir ! *(Thank you very much and goodbye!)* | Invariable | Often pronounced [oʁvwaʁ] ('or-vwar') in rapid speech. |
| **enchanté / enchantée** | Nice to meet you / Delighted | `ahn-shahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=enchant%C3%A9%20/%20enchant%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Enchanté de faire votre connaissance. *(Delighted to meet you.)* | enchantés / enchantées | Pronounced identically [ɑ̃ʃɑ̃te]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **s'il vous plaît** | Please (formal/plural) | `seel voo PLEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27il%20vous%20pla%C3%AEt&op=translate) | `Expression` | Un café et l'addition, s'il vous plaît. *(A coffee and the bill, please.)* | Informal: s'il te plaît | Silent 't' in plaît [sil vu plɛ]. Liaison: s'il‿vous [sil-vu]. |
| **s'il te plaît** | Please (informal) | `seel tuh PLEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27il%20te%20pla%C3%AEt&op=translate) | `Expression` | Passe-moi le sel, s'il te plaît. *(Pass me the salt, please.)* | Formal: s'il vous plaît | Use with children, friends, and close peers. |
| **merci** | Thank you | `mair-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=merci&op=translate) | `Expression` | Merci pour votre accueil chaleureux. *(Thank you for your warm welcome.)* | Invariable | Pronounced [mɛʁsi]. Clear trilled or uvular 'r'. |
| **merci beaucoup** | Thank you very much | `mair-SEE boh-KOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=merci%20beaucoup&op=translate) | `Expression` | Merci beaucoup pour ce beau cadeau ! *(Thank you very much for this beautiful gift!)* | Invariable | Final 'p' in beaucoup is strictly silent [boku]. |
| **de rien** | You're welcome (informal) | `duh RYA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=de%20rien&op=translate) | `Expression` | — Merci pour ton aide. — De rien ! *(— Thanks for your help. — You're welcome!)* | Invariable | Nasal vowel [ʁjɛ̃]. Common everyday response. |
| **je vous en prie** | You're welcome / Please (formal) | `zhuh voo-zahn PREE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=je%20vous%20en%20prie&op=translate) | `Expression` | Entrez, je vous en prie. *(Please come in, I insist.)* | Informal: je t'en prie | Mandatory [z] liaison: vous‿en [vu-z-ɑ̃]. |
| **je t'en prie** | You're welcome (informal) | `zhuh tahn PREE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=je%20t%27en%20prie&op=translate) | `Expression` | Je t'en prie, c'est tout naturel. *(You're welcome, it's quite natural.)* | Formal: je vous en prie | Elision: te + en = t'en [tɑ̃]. |
| **pardon** | Pardon / Excuse me / Sorry | `par-DOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pardon&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Pardon monsieur, où est la gare ? *(Excuse me sir, where is the station?)* | les pardons | Nasal vowel [dɔ̃]. Used to pass by or ask forgiveness. |
| **excusez-moi** | Excuse me (formal/plural) | `ek-skyoo-ZAY MWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=excusez-moi&op=translate) | `Expression` | Excusez-moi, avez-vous l'heure ? *(Excuse me, do you have the time?)* | Informal: excuse-moi | Imperative of excuser with pronoun moi. |
| **excuse-moi** | Excuse me (informal) | `ek-SKYOOS MWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=excuse-moi&op=translate) | `Expression` | Excuse-moi, je suis un peu en retard. *(Excuse me, I'm a bit late.)* | Formal: excusez-moi | Silent final 'e' in excuse [ɛkskyz]. |
| **bonne journée** | Have a nice day | `buhn zhoor-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bonne%20journ%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Au revoir et bonne journée à vous ! *(Goodbye and have a good day to you!)* | les bonnes journées | Said when leaving during daytime. |
| **bonne soirée** | Have a pleasant evening | `buhn swah-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bonne%20soir%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Merci pour le dîner, bonne soirée ! *(Thank you for dinner, have a good evening!)* | les bonnes soirées | Said when parting after ~18:00. |
| **bonne nuit** | Good night (before bed) | `buhn NWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bonne%20nuit&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il est tard, je vais dormir. Bonne nuit ! *(It's late, I'm going to sleep. Good night!)* | les bonnes nuits | Silent 't' in nuit [nɥi]. Only said right before sleep. |
| **à bientôt** | See you soon | `ah byan-TOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20bient%C3%B4t&op=translate) | `Expression` | C'était un grand plaisir, à bientôt ! *(It was a great pleasure, see you soon!)* | Invariable | Silent final 't' in bientôt [bjɛ̃to]. |
| **à demain** | See you tomorrow | `ah duh-MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20demain&op=translate) | `Expression` | Bonne soirée et à demain au bureau ! *(Have a good evening and see you tomorrow at the office!)* | Invariable | Nasal vowel in demain [dəmɛ̃]. |
| **à plus tard** | See you later | `ah plyoo TAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20plus%20tard&op=translate) | `Expression` | Je dois partir, à plus tard ! *(I must go, see you later!)* | Invariable | Shortened to 'à plus' [a plys] in casual slang. |
| **à tout à l'heure** | See you in a bit (same day) | `ah too tah LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20tout%20%C3%A0%20l%27heure&op=translate) | `Expression` | On se voit après le cours, à tout à l'heure ! *(See you after class, see you in a bit!)* | Invariable | Mandatory [t] liaison: tout‿à [tu-t-a]. |
| **bienvenue** | Welcome | `byan-vuh-NYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bienvenue&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Bienvenue dans notre maison ! *(Welcome to our home!)* | les bienvenues | As noun: la bienvenue. Plural adjective: bienvenus. |
| **comment allez-vous ?** | How are you? (formal) | `koh-mahn-tah-lay-VOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=comment%20allez-vous%20%3F&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb` | Bonjour docteur, comment allez-vous ? *(Hello doctor, how are you?)* | Verb aller with vous | Mandatory [t] liaison: comment‿allez [kɔmɑ̃.t‿ale]. |
| **comment vas-tu ?** | How are you? (informal) | `koh-mahn vah-TYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=comment%20vas-tu%20%3F&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb` | Salut Julien, comment vas-tu ? *(Hi Julien, how are you?)* | Verb aller with tu | No liaison between comment and vas. |
| **ça va** | How are things? / I'm fine | `sah VAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A7a%20va&op=translate) | `Expression` | — Ça va ? — Oui, ça va très bien ! *(— How are things? — Yes, very well!)* | Invariable | Cedilla (ç) sounds as [s] before 'a'. |
| **très bien** | Very well | `tray BYA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=tr%C3%A8s%20bien&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Tout se passe très bien ici. *(Everything is going very well here.)* | Invariable | Liaison: très‿occupé [tʁɛ-z-ɔkype] with [z]. |
| **bon courage** | Good luck / Hang in there | `bohn koo-RAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20courage&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Tu as des examens ? Bon courage ! *(You have exams? Hang in there!)* | Invariable in greeting | Nouns in -age are masculine (le courage). |
| **félicitations** | Congratulations | `fay-lee-see-tah-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=f%C3%A9licitations&op=translate) | `Feminine Plural (f. pl.)` | Félicitations pour votre réussite ! *(Congratulations on your success!)* | Always used in plural | Feminine plural noun. Nasal [sjɔ̃]. |
| **santé !** | Cheers! / To your health! | `sahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sant%C3%A9%20%21&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Levons nos verres : à votre santé ! *(Let's raise our glasses: to your health!)* | la santé / les santés | Said when toasting. Also: 'À la vôtre !'. |

## 2. Self-Introduction and Identity (Présentation et identité)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le nom** | Last name / Family name | `luh NOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20nom&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon nom de famille est Martin. *(My family name is Martin.)* | les noms | Nasal [ɔ̃], silent 'm'. On forms, 'Nom' = surname. |
| **le prénom** | First name / Given name | `luh pray-NOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pr%C3%A9nom&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon prénom est Thomas. *(My first name is Thomas.)* | les prénoms | False friend: 'prénom' = FIRST name, not last. |
| **le surnom** | Nickname | `luh soor-NOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20surnom&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mes amis me donnent le surnom de Tom. *(My friends give me the nickname Tom.)* | les surnoms | False friend: 'surnom' is nickname, NOT surname. |
| **s'appeler** | To be named / To be called | `sah-puh-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27appeler&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb` | Je m'appelle Sophie et j'habite à Lille. *(My name is Sophie and I live in Lille.)* | je m'appelle, tu t'appelles, il s'appelle, nous nous appelons, vous vous appelez, ils s'appellent | Spelling change: doubles 'l' except with nous & vous. |
| **être** | To be | `EH-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre&op=translate) | `Irregular Verb` | Je suis ravi de faire votre connaissance. *(I am delighted to meet you.)* | je suis, tu es, il est, nous sommes, vous êtes, ils sont | Liaison: vous‿êtes [vu-z-ɛt], c'est‿un [sɛ-t-œ̃]. |
| **avoir** | To have | `ah-VWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir&op=translate) | `Irregular Verb` | J'ai vingt-huit ans. *(I am twenty-eight years old.)* | j'ai, tu as, il a, nous avons, vous avez, ils ont | Use avoir for age! Liaisons: nous‿avons [z], ils‿ont [z]. |
| **l'âge** | Age | `LAZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A2ge&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel est votre âge, s'il vous plaît ? *(What is your age, please?)* | les âges [lay-ZAHZH] | Masculine noun despite ending in -e. Elision: l'âge. |
| **l'an** | Year (unit for age) | `LAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27an&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon frère a trente ans. *(My brother is thirty years old.)* | les ans | Mandatory to say 'ans' for age (never 'J'ai 30'). |
| **l'anniversaire** | Birthday / Anniversary | `lah-nee-vair-SAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27anniversaire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon anniversaire est le 10 mars. *(My birthday is March 10th.)* | les anniversaires | Masculine ending in -e. Liaison: Joyeux‿anniversaire [z]. |
| **la date de naissance** | Date of birth | `lah daht duh neh-SAHNS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20date%20de%20naissance&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Indiquez votre date de naissance. *(Indicate your date of birth.)* | les dates de naissance | Takes masculine article 'le' before dates: le 4 mai. |
| **le lieu de naissance** | Place of birth | `luh lyuh duh neh-SAHNS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lieu%20de%20naissance&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Son lieu de naissance est Bordeaux. *(His place of birth is Bordeaux.)* | les lieux de naissance | Irregular plural: le lieu -> les lieux (takes -x). |
| **l'adresse** | Address | `lah-DRES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27adresse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Voici mon adresse électronique. *(Here is my email address.)* | les adresses | Feminine noun. Before vowel: mon adresse (not *ma). |
| **le numéro de téléphone** | Telephone number | `luh nyoo-may-ROH duh tay-lay-FOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20num%C3%A9ro%20de%20t%C3%A9l%C3%A9phone&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel est votre numéro de téléphone ? *(What is your phone number?)* | les numéros de téléphone | Read in pairs in French: 06 12 34 56 78. |
| **le courriel** | Email / Email address | `luh koor-YEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20courriel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Envoyez-moi un courriel avec vos coordonnées. *(Send me an email with your contact info.)* | les courriels | Official French term for email (also: un e-mail). |
| **la nationalité** | Nationality | `lah nah-syoh-nah-lee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20nationalit%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle est votre nationalité ? *(What is your nationality?)* | les nationalités | Nouns ending in -ité are consistently feminine. |
| **le pays** | Country | `luh pay-EE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pays&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La France est mon pays d'adoption. *(France is my adopted country.)* | les pays (invariable) | Two syllables [pe.i]. Invariable in plural (ends in -s). |
| **la ville** | City / Town | `lah VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20ville&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Paris est une grande ville historique. *(Paris is a large historic city.)* | les villes | Pronunciation exception: 'll' is [l], not [j]: [vil]. |
| **habiter** | To live / To reside | `ah-bee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=habiter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | J'habite à Lyon depuis trois ans. *(I have been living in Lyon for three years.)* | j'habite, tu habites, il habite, nous habitons, vous habitez, ils habitent | Mute 'h'. Elision: j'habite. Plural liaisons [z]. |
| **venir de** | To come from | `vuh-neer DUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=venir%20de&op=translate) | `Verb (3rd group)` | Je viens du Canada. *(I come from Canada.)* | je viens, tu viens, il vient, nous venons, vous venez, ils viennent | Contraction: de + le = du (venir du Japon). |
| **la profession** | Profession / Occupation | `lah proh-feh-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20profession&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle est votre profession actuelle ? *(What is your current occupation?)* | les professions | Nouns in -sion are always feminine. |
| **l'état civil** | Marital status | `lay-tah see-VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9tat%20civil&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Remplissez la case état civil. *(Fill in the marital status box.)* | les états civils | Elision: l'état. Silent 't' in état [eta]. |
| **célibataire** | Single / Unmarried | `say-lee-bah-TAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=c%C3%A9libataire&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Lucas est célibataire et vit seul. *(Lucas is single and lives alone.)* | célibataires | Epicene adjective: identical for masculine and feminine. |
| **marié / mariée** | Married | `mah-RYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mari%C3%A9%20/%20mari%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ils sont mariés depuis dix ans. *(They have been married for ten years.)* | mariés / mariées | Pronounced identically [maʁje]. Feminine adds '-e'. |
| **la signature** | Signature | `lah see-nyah-TYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20signature&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Apposez votre signature en bas du document. *(Put your signature at the bottom of the document.)* | les signatures | Nouns ending in -ure are predominantly feminine. |
| **la carte d'identité** | ID card | `lah kart dee-dahn-tee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20carte%20d%27identit%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Montrez votre carte d'identité au contrôle. *(Show your ID card at the checkpoint.)* | les cartes d'identité | Elision: d'identité. Feminine noun. |
| **le passeport** | Passport | `luh pahs-POR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20passeport&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon passeport est valide pour dix ans. *(My passport is valid for ten years.)* | les passeports | Silent final 't' [paspɔʁ]. |
| **la personne** | Person | `lah pair-SUHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20personne&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Cette personne est très compétente. *(This person is very competent.)* | les personnes | Always feminine, even when referring to a male. |
| **l'homme** | Man | `LUHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27homme&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Cet homme est mon professeur de musique. *(This man is my music teacher.)* | les hommes [lay-ZOHM] | Mute 'h': forces elision (l'homme) and [z] liaison. |
| **la femme** | Woman / Wife | `lah FAHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20femme&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | C'est une femme remarquable. *(She is a remarkable woman.)* | les femmes | Pronunciation trap: spelled 'e' but pronounced [fam]. |
| **l'enfant** | Child | `lahn-FAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27enfant&op=translate) | `Masculine / Feminine (m./f.)` | L'enfant joue tranquillement au salon. *(The child plays quietly in the living room.)* | les enfants [lay-zahn-FAHN] | Two nasal vowels [ɑ̃fɑ̃]. Plural liaison with [z]. |

## 3. Numbers 0 to 100 (Les nombres de 0 à 100)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **zéro** | Zero (0) | `zay-ROH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=z%C3%A9ro&op=translate) | `Numeral` | La température est de zéro degré ce matin. *(The temperature is zero degrees this morning.)* | Invariable | Clear [z] sound at start: [zeʁo]. |
| **un / une** | One (1) | `UH(N) / YOON` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20/%20une&op=translate) | `Numeral (m./f.)` | J'ai un frère et une sœur. *(I have one brother and one sister.)* | des | Only numeral with distinct feminine form (une). |
| **deux** | Two (2) | `DUH [dø]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=deux&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il a deux enfants magnifiques. *(He has two magnificent children.)* | Invariable | Alone: silent 'x'. Liaison with [z]: deux‿ans [dø-z-ɑ̃]. |
| **trois** | Three (3) | `TRWAH [tʁwa]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=trois&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Nous restons trois jours à Paris. *(We stay three days in Paris.)* | Invariable | Alone: silent 's'. Liaison with [z]: trois‿heures [tʁwa-z-œʁ]. |
| **quatre** | Four (4) | `KAH-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quatre&op=translate) | `Numeral` | La table a quatre chaises. *(The table has four chairs.)* | Invariable | Pronounced [katʁ]. Final 'e' drops in fast speech. |
| **cinq** | Five (5) | `SA(N)K [sɛ̃k]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=cinq&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Le magasin ouvre dans cinq minutes. *(The store opens in five minutes.)* | Invariable | Final 'q' is sounded: [sɛ̃k]. |
| **six** | Six (6) | `SEES [sis]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=six&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Nous partons à six heures du matin. *(We leave at six in the morning.)* | Invariable | Alone: [sis]. Before consonant: [si]. Liaison: [z] six‿ans. |
| **sept** | Seven (7) | `SET [sɛt]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sept&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il y a sept jours dans une semaine. *(There are seven days in a week.)* | Invariable | The 'p' is silent, 't' is sounded: pronounced [sɛt]. |
| **huit** | Eight (8) | `WEET [ɥit]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=huit&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Le cours commence à huit heures. *(The class begins at eight o'clock.)* | Invariable | Aspired 'h' (*h aspiré*): no elision (le huit mai). |
| **neuf** | Nine (9) | `NUHF [nœf]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=neuf&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Elle a neuf ans aujourd'hui. *(She is nine years old today.)* | Invariable | Liaison with ans/heures turns 'f' into [v]: neuf‿ans [nœ-v-ɑ̃]. |
| **dix** | Ten (10) | `DEES [dis]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dix&op=translate) | `Numeral` | J'ai dix euros dans mon porte-monnaie. *(I have ten euros in my wallet.)* | Invariable | Alone: [dis]. Before consonant: [di]. Liaison: [z] dix‿euros. |
| **onze** | Eleven (11) | `OHNZ [ɔ̃z]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=onze&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Le rendez-vous est fixé à onze heures. *(The appointment is set for eleven o'clock.)* | Invariable | Treated as consonant onset: NO elision (*le onze mai*, not *l'onze). |
| **douze** | Twelve (12) | `DOOZ [duz]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=douze&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Une année compte douze mois. *(A year has twelve months.)* | Invariable | Pronounced [duz]. |
| **treize** | Thirteen (13) | `TREHZ [tʁɛz]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=treize&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Le numéro treize porte bonheur en France. *(Number thirteen brings good luck in France.)* | Invariable | Pronounced [tʁɛz]. |
| **quatorze** | Fourteen (14) | `kah-TORZ [katɔʁz]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quatorze&op=translate) | `Numeral` | La fête nationale est le quatorze juillet. *(The national holiday is July 14th.)* | Invariable | Pronounced with [k], not English [kw]. |
| **quinze** | Fifteen (15) | `KA(N)Z [kɛ̃z]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quinze&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il est quinze heures trente. *(It is 3:30 PM.)* | Invariable | Nasal [kɛ̃z]. Note: 'quinze jours' = a fortnight / 2 weeks. |
| **seize** | Sixteen (16) | `SEHZ [sɛz]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=seize&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Ma fille fête ses seize ans. *(My daughter celebrates her 16th birthday.)* | Invariable | Pronounced [sɛz]. Last numeral ending in -ze. |
| **dix-sept** | Seventeen (17) | `dees-SET [dis.sɛt]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dix-sept&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Ce livre coûte dix-sept euros. *(This book costs seventeen euros.)* | Invariable | Compound number with hyphen. Pronounced [dissɛt]. |
| **dix-huit** | Eighteen (18) | `deez-WEET [di.zɥit]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dix-huit&op=translate) | `Numeral` | On est majeur à dix-huit ans. *(One is of legal age at eighteen.)* | Invariable | The 'x' sounds like [z]. Mandatory [t] liaison: dix-huit‿ans. |
| **dix-neuf** | Nineteen (19) | `deez-NUHF [diz.nœf]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dix-neuf&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il a dix-neuf ans et étudie le droit. *(He is nineteen years old and studies law.)* | Invariable | The 'x' sounds like [z] before nasal: [diznœf]. |
| **vingt** | Twenty (20) | `VA(N) [vɛ̃]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vingt&op=translate) | `Numeral` | J'ai exactement vingt ans aujourd'hui. *(I am exactly twenty years old today.)* | Invariable | Alone: silent 'gt'. Liaison: vingt‿ans [vɛ̃-t-ɑ̃] with [t] bridge! |
| **vingt et un** | Twenty-one (21) | `va(n)-tay-UH(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vingt%20et%20un&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il a vingt et un ans. *(He is twenty-one years old.)* | Invariable | Uses 'et'. Forbidden liaison after 'et': say [e] \| [œ̃]. |
| **trente** | Thirty (30) | `TRAHNT [tʁɑ̃t]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=trente&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Mon frère a trente ans. *(My brother is thirty years old.)* | Invariable | Liaison: trente‿ans [tʁɑ̃-t-ɑ̃]. |
| **trente et un** | Thirty-one (31) | `trahn-tay-UH(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=trente%20et%20un&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Le mois compte trente et un jours. *(The month has thirty-one days.)* | Invariable | Connects with 'et'. No liaison after 'et'. |
| **quarante** | Forty (40) | `kah-RAHNT [kaʁɑ̃t]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quarante&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Notre professeur a quarante ans. *(Our teacher is forty years old.)* | Invariable | Spelled 'qu' but pronounced [k], not [kw]. |
| **cinquante** | Fifty (50) | `sa(n)-KAHNT [sɛ̃kɑ̃t]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=cinquante&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Ma mère fête ses cinquante ans. *(My mother celebrates her fiftieth birthday.)* | Invariable | Initial 'c' is [s], 'qu' is [k]. |
| **soixante** | Sixty (60) | `swah-SAHNT [swasɑ̃t]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=soixante&op=translate) | `Numeral` | La vitesse est limitée à soixante kilomètres à l'heure. *(Speed is limited to 60 km/h.)* | Invariable | Pronunciation trap: 'x' is pronounced [s] [swasɑ̃t]. |
| **soixante-dix** | Seventy (70) | `swah-sahnt-DEES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=soixante-dix&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Mon grand-père a soixante-dix ans. *(My grandfather is seventy years old.)* | Invariable | Base 20 system: 60 + 10 = 70. 71 is soixante et onze. |
| **quatre-vingts** | Eighty (80) | `kah-truh-VA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quatre-vingts&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Ce monsieur a quatre-vingts ans. *(This gentleman is eighty years old.)* | Takes -s alone; drops -s when followed by a number (quatre-vingt-cinq) | Liaison: quatre-vingts‿ans [katʁəvɛ̃-z-ɑ̃] with [z]. |
| **cent** | One hundred (100) | `SAHN [sɑ̃]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=cent&op=translate) | `Numeral` | Il y a cent personnes dans la salle. *(There are one hundred people in the room.)* | Takes -s when multiplied (deux cents) without trailing numbers | Liaison: cent‿euros [sɑ̃-t-øʁo] with [t] bridge. |

## 4. Days of the Week and Months of the Year (Les jours et les mois)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le lundi** | Monday | `luh luhn-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lundi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le lundi, je commence à huit heures. *(On Mondays, I start at eight o'clock.)* | les lundis | All days of the week are masculine and lowercase in French. |
| **le mardi** | Tuesday | `luh mar-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mardi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous avons cours de français le mardi. *(We have French class on Tuesdays.)* | les mardis | 'Le mardi' means every Tuesday; 'mardi' means this Tuesday. |
| **le mercredi** | Wednesday | `luh mair-kruh-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mercredi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les enfants ne vont pas à l'école le mercredi après-midi. *(Children don't go to school Wednesday afternoon.)* | les mercredis | Traditional half-day in French schools. |
| **le jeudi** | Thursday | `luh zhuh-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jeudi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je fais du sport tous les jeudis soir. *(I exercise every Thursday evening.)* | les jeudis | Pronounced with soft [ʒ] sound [ʒødi]. |
| **le vendredi** | Friday | `luh vahn-druh-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vendredi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le vendredi soir, je me repose enfin. *(On Friday evenings, I finally rest.)* | les vendredis | Nasal vowel [vɑ̃dʁədi]. |
| **le samedi** | Saturday | `luh sahm-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20samedi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le samedi, nous faisons les courses au marché. *(On Saturdays, we shop at the market.)* | les samedis | The middle 'e' drops in natural speech: [samdi]. |
| **le dimanche** | Sunday | `luh dee-MAHNSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dimanche&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le dimanche est un jour de repos en famille. *(Sunday is a day of rest with family.)* | les dimanches | Nasal [ɑ̃] + [ʃ]: [dimɑ̃ʃ]. |
| **la semaine** | Week | `lah suh-MEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20semaine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je travaille quarante heures par semaine. *(I work forty hours per week.)* | les semaines | Pronounced [səmɛn]. 'En semaine' = on weekdays. |
| **le week-end** | Weekend | `luh week-END` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20week-end&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Qu'est-ce que tu fais pendant le week-end ? *(What are you doing over the weekend?)* | les week-ends | Masculine borrowed noun. Widely used across France. |
| **janvier** | January | `zhahn-VYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=janvier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'année commence en janvier. *(The year begins in January.)* | All months masculine | All months are lowercase in French. Preposition: 'en janvier'. |
| **février** | February | `fay-vree-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=f%C3%A9vrier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Février est le mois le plus court. *(February is the shortest month.)* | Masculine | Accent aigu on 'é': [fevʁije]. |
| **mars** | March | `MAHRS [maʁs]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mars&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le printemps arrive à la fin du mois de mars. *(Spring arrives at the end of March.)* | Masculine | Pronunciation exception: the final 's' IS sounded: [maʁs]. |
| **avril** | April | `ah-VREEL [avʁil]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avril&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En avril, ne te découvre pas d'un fil. *(In April, do not shed a thread - proverb.)* | Masculine | The final 'l' is clearly pronounced: [avʁil]. |
| **mai** | May | `MEH [mɛ]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mai&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le premier mai est la fête du travail. *(May 1st is Labor Day.)* | Masculine | Pronounced [mɛ]. Do not confuse with 'mais' (but). |
| **juin** | June | `ZHWA(N) [ʒwɛ̃]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=juin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'été commence officiellement en juin. *(Summer officially begins in June.)* | Masculine | Nasal vowel [ʒwɛ̃]. Compare carefully with juillet. |
| **juillet** | July | `zhwee-YEH [ʒɥijɛ]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=juillet&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La fête nationale française a lieu le 14 juillet. *(Bastille Day takes place on July 14th.)* | Masculine | Glide [j] + open [ɛ]; silent 't'. |
| **août** | August | `OOT [ut]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ao%C3%BBt&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Beaucoup d'entreprises ferment en août. *(Many businesses close in August.)* | Masculine | Pronounced [ut] with clear 't' sound. |
| **septembre** | September | `sep-TAHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=septembre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La rentrée des classes a lieu en septembre. *(The return to school takes place in September.)* | Masculine | The 'p' IS pronounced here: [sɛptɑ̃bʁ] (unlike 'sept'). |
| **octobre** | October | `ok-TOH-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=octobre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les feuilles rougissent en octobre. *(Leaves turn red in October.)* | Masculine | Pronounced [ɔktɔbʁ]. |
| **novembre** | November | `noh-VAHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=novembre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le climat devient frais en novembre. *(The weather turns chilly in November.)* | Masculine | Pronounced [nɔvɑ̃bʁ]. |
| **décembre** | December | `day-SAHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9cembre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Noël se fête le 25 décembre. *(Christmas is celebrated on December 25th.)* | Masculine | Accent aigu: [desɑ̃bʁ]. |
| **le jour** | Day | `luh ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jour&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel jour sommes-nous aujourd'hui ? *(What day is it today?)* | les jours | 'Jour' is a count unit; 'journée' emphasizes duration. |
| **la journée** | Daytime / Whole day | `lah zhoor-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20journ%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai passé une excellente journée. *(I spent an excellent day.)* | les journées | Feminine noun ending in -ée. |
| **le mois** | Month | `luh MWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mois&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il y a douze mois dans une année. *(There are twelve months in a year.)* | les mois (invariable) | Invariable in plural (ends in -s). Liaison: des mois‿entiers [z]. |
| **l'année** | Year (duration) | `lah-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ann%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Bonne année et bonne santé ! *(Happy New Year and good health!)* | les années | Feminine noun. Elision: l'année. |
| **aujourd'hui** | Today | `oh-zhoor-DWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=aujourd%27hui&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Aujourd'hui, il fait très beau dehors. *(Today, the weather is very nice outside.)* | Invariable | Note apostrophe: [oʒuʁdɥi]. |
| **demain** | Tomorrow | `duh-MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=demain&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Demain matin, je dois me lever tôt. *(Tomorrow morning, I have to wake up early.)* | Invariable | Nasal vowel [dəmɛ̃]. |
| **hier** | Yesterday | `YAIR [jɛʁ]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=hier&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Hier soir, nous sommes allés au cinéma. *(Yesterday evening, we went to the movies.)* | Invariable | Mute 'h'. Pronounced [jɛʁ]. |
| **la date** | Date (calendar) | `lah DAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20date&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle est la date d'aujourd'hui ? *(What is today's date?)* | les dates | Feminine noun. |
| **le calendrier** | Calendar | `luh kah-lahn-dree-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20calendrier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je note la réunion sur mon calendrier. *(I write down the meeting on my calendar.)* | les calendriers | Silent final 'r' in -er: [kalɑ̃dʁije]. |

## 5. Telling the Time (Dire l'heure)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **l'heure** | Hour / Time | `LUHR [œʁ]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27heure&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle heure est-il, s'il vous plaît ? *(What time is it, please?)* | les heures [lay-ZUHR] | Feminine. Mute 'h'. Liaison with numbers: deux‿heures [dø-z-œʁ]. |
| **la minute** | Minute | `lah mee-NYOOT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20minute&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Attendez une minute, j'arrive ! *(Wait a minute, I'm coming!)* | les minutes | Feminine noun [minyt]. |
| **la seconde** | Second | `lah suh-GOHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20seconde&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le chronomètre compte chaque seconde. *(The stopwatch counts every second.)* | les secondes | Pronunciation exception: the 'c' is voiced as [ɡ] [səɡɔ̃d]! |
| **midi** | Noon / Midday (12:00 PM) | `mee-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=midi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous déjeunons ensemble à midi. *(We have lunch together at noon.)* | Invariable | Say 'Il est midi' (never *il est douze heures). |
| **minuit** | Midnight (12:00 AM) | `mee-NWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=minuit&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le feu d'artifice commence à minuit. *(The fireworks begin at midnight.)* | Invariable | Silent 't' [minɥi]. Say 'Il est minuit'. |
| **le matin** | Morning | `luh mah-TA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20matin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je prends un café noir le matin. *(I have black coffee in the morning.)* | les matins | 'Le matin' = every morning. Nasal [matɛ̃]. |
| **la matinée** | Morning duration / Entire morning | `lah mah-tee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20matin%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai travaillé toute la matinée. *(I worked all morning long.)* | les matinées | Emphasizes the unfolding duration of morning. |
| **l'après-midi** | Afternoon | `lah-preh-mee-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27apr%C3%A8s-midi&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Cet après-midi, nous avons une réunion importante. *(This afternoon, we have an important meeting.)* | les après-midis | Compound noun. Masculine in modern standard French. |
| **le soir** | Evening | `luh SWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20soir&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le soir, j'aime lire au calme. *(In the evening, I like reading quietly.)* | les soirs | 'Le soir' = every evening. Masculine noun. |
| **la soirée** | Evening duration / Entire evening | `lah swah-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20soir%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous avons passé une très bonne soirée. *(We spent a very good evening.)* | les soirées | Feminine. Also means evening party/gathering. |
| **la nuit** | Night | `lah NWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20nuit&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La nuit tombe tôt en hiver. *(Night falls early in winter.)* | les nuits | Silent final 't' [nɥi]. Feminine noun. |
| **et quart** | Quarter past | `ay KAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=et%20quart&op=translate) | `Expression` | Il est huit heures et quart. *(It is a quarter past eight.)* | Invariable | Use 'et quart' (with 'et'). Silent 't' in quart [kaʁ]. |
| **et demie** | Half past | `ay duh-MEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=et%20demie&op=translate) | `Expression` | Le train part à neuf heures et demie. *(The train departs at half past nine.)* | Takes -e with heure (feminine: demie) | Agreement: 'heure' is feminine, so 'demie' takes '-e'. |
| **moins le quart** | Quarter to | `mwa(n) luh KAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=moins%20le%20quart&op=translate) | `Expression` | Il est dix heures moins le quart. *(It is a quarter to ten.)* | Invariable | Notice the article 'le' in 'moins le quart'. |
| **pile** | On the dot / Exactly | `PEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pile&op=translate) | `Adverb` | La réunion commence à dix heures pile. *(The meeting begins at ten o'clock sharp.)* | Invariable | Colloquial and universal expression: 'dix heures pile'. |
| **tôt** | Early | `TOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=t%C3%B4t&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Je me réveille très tôt le lundi. *(I wake up very early on Mondays.)* | Invariable | Circumflex on 'ô' [to]. Silent 't'. |
| **tard** | Late | `TAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=tard&op=translate) | `Adverb` | Il est rentré très tard hier soir. *(He returned very late last night.)* | Invariable | Silent final 'd' [taʁ]. |
| **en avance** | Early / Ahead of time | `ahn-nah-VAHNS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=en%20avance&op=translate) | `Adverbial phrase` | Le train est arrivé en avance de cinq minutes. *(The train arrived five minutes early.)* | Invariable | Mandatory [n] liaison: en‿avance [ɑ̃-n-avɑ̃s]. |
| **à l'heure** | On time | `ah LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20l%27heure&op=translate) | `Adverbial phrase` | L'avion a atterri exactement à l'heure. *(The plane landed exactly on time.)* | Invariable | Elision: à l'heure. |
| **en retard** | Late / Delayed | `ahn ruh-TAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=en%20retard&op=translate) | `Adverbial phrase` | Pardonnez-moi, je suis en retard. *(Forgive me, I am running late.)* | Invariable | Silent 'd' in retard [ʁətaʁ]. |
| **la montre** | Watch (wristwatch) | `lah MOHN-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20montre&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma montre avance de deux minutes. *(My watch is two minutes fast.)* | les montres | Feminine noun [mɔ̃tʁ]. |
| **l'horloge** | Clock (wall clock) | `lor-LOHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27horloge&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | L'horloge de la mairie sonne chaque heure. *(The town hall clock rings every hour.)* | les horloges | Feminine noun. Mute 'h': l'horloge. |
| **le réveil** | Alarm clock | `luh ray-VEYE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20r%C3%A9veil&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon réveil sonne à six heures trente. *(My alarm clock rings at 6:30.)* | les réveils | Pronounced with liquid [j] [ʁevɛj]. |
| **sonner** | To ring | `soh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sonner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Le téléphone sonne sans arrêt. *(The phone rings non-stop.)* | je sonne, tu sonnes, il sonne, nous sonnons, vous sonnez, ils sonnent | Regular -er verb [sɔne]. |
| **durer** | To last | `dyoo-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=durer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Le film dure deux heures et demie. *(The movie lasts two and a half hours.)* | je dure, tu dures, il dure, nous durons, vous durez, ils durent | Regular -er verb [dyʁe]. |
| **passer le temps** | To pass the time | `pah-SAY luh TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=passer%20le%20temps&op=translate) | `Verb phrase` | Je lis pour passer le temps dans le train. *(I read to pass the time on the train.)* | Uses verb passer | Silent 's' in temps [tɑ̃]. |
| **perdre du temps** | To waste time | `PAIR-druh dyoo TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=perdre%20du%20temps&op=translate) | `Verb phrase` | Ne perds pas de temps avec ça. *(Don't waste time with that.)* | Uses verb perdre | Negation takes 'de': ne perds pas de temps. |
| **gagner du temps** | To save time | `gah-NYAY dyoo TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gagner%20du%20temps&op=translate) | `Verb phrase` | Prendre le métro permet de gagner du temps. *(Taking the metro saves time.)* | Uses verb gagner | French says 'gagner du temps' (literally win time). |
| **le moment** | Moment | `luh moh-MAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20moment&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Attendez un moment, s'il vous plaît. *(Wait a moment, please.)* | les moments | Silent 't' in moment [mɔmɑ̃]. |
| **l'instant** | Instant / Immediate moment | `la(n)-STAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27instant&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un instant, je vérifie mon agenda. *(One moment, I am checking my schedule.)* | les instants [lay-zahn-STAHN] | Masculine noun. Silent final 't' [ɛ̃stɑ̃]. Plural [z]. |

## 6. Seasons and Weather (Les saisons et la météo)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la saison** | Season | `lah seh-ZOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20saison&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le printemps est ma saison préférée. *(Spring is my favorite season.)* | les saisons | Feminine noun [sɛzɔ̃]. |
| **le printemps** | Spring | `luh pra(n)-TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20printemps&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Au printemps, les fleurs s'épanouissent. *(In spring, flowers bloom.)* | les printemps | Preposition exception: 'AU printemps' (not *en printemps)! |
| **l'été** | Summer | `lay-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9t%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En été, il fait très chaud sur la côte. *(In summer, it is very hot on the coast.)* | les étés | Preposition: 'en été'. Masculine noun. |
| **l'automne** | Autumn / Fall | `loh-TUHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27automne&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En automne, le temps devient pluvieux. *(In autumn, the weather turns rainy.)* | les automnes | Silent 'm' in automne: pronounced [otɔn]. Preposition: 'en automne'. |
| **l'hiver** | Winter | `lee-VAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27hiver&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En hiver, il neige souvent dans les Alpes. *(In winter, it often snows in the Alps.)* | les hivers | Mute 'h'. Preposition: 'en hiver'. Final 'r' is sounded [ivɛʁ]. |
| **la météo** | Weather forecast | `lah may-tay-OH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20m%C3%A9t%C3%A9o&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Regardons la météo avant de partir en randonnée. *(Let's check the forecast before going hiking.)* | les météos | Short for la météorologie. Feminine. |
| **le temps** | Weather / Time | `luh TAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20temps&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel temps fait-il aujourd'hui ? *(What is the weather like today?)* | les temps (invariable) | Silent final 'ps' [tɑ̃]. Weather uses verb 'faire': il fait beau. |
| **le soleil** | Sun | `luh soh-LEYE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20soleil&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le soleil brille dans le ciel bleu. *(The sun shines in the blue sky.)* | les soleils | Collocation: 'Il y a du soleil' (partitive). |
| **la pluie** | Rain | `lah PLWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20pluie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La pluie tombe sans discontinuer. *(The rain falls without stopping.)* | les pluies | Collocation: 'Il y a de la pluie' / 'Il pleut'. |
| **le vent** | Wind | `luh VAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vent&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il y a beaucoup de vent aujourd'hui. *(There is a lot of wind today.)* | les vents | Silent 't' [vɑ̃]. Collocation: 'Il y a du vent'. |
| **la neige** | Snow | `lah NEHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20neige&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La neige recouvre les toits du village. *(Snow covers the village roofs.)* | les neiges | Collocation: 'Il y a de la neige' / 'Il neige'. |
| **le nuage** | Cloud | `luh noo-AHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20nuage&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le ciel est couvert de gros nuages gris. *(The sky is covered with big gray clouds.)* | les nuages | Masculine noun in -age. 'Nuageux' = cloudy. |
| **l'orage** | Storm / Thunderstorm | `loh-RAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27orage&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un violent orage éclate en fin de journée. *(A violent thunderstorm breaks out at end of day.)* | les orages | Masculine noun ending in -age. Elision: l'orage. |
| **l'éclair** | Lightning / Lightning bolt | `lay-KLAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9clair&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un éclair illumine le ciel nocturne. *(A lightning flash illuminates the night sky.)* | les éclairs | Masculine. Also means the famous chocolate pastry! |
| **le tonnerre** | Thunder | `luh toh-NAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20tonnerre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'entends le bruit sourd du tonnerre. *(I hear the deep rumble of thunder.)* | les tonnerres | Masculine noun [tɔnɛʁ]. Double 'n'. |
| **le brouillard** | Fog / Mist | `luh broo-YAR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20brouillard&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il y a un épais brouillard sur la route. *(There is thick fog on the road.)* | les brouillards | Silent final 'd' [bʁujaʁ]. Liquid 'll' [j]. |
| **pleuvoir** | To rain | `pluh-VWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pleuvoir&op=translate) | `Impersonal Verb` | Prends ton parapluie, il pleut dehors ! *(Take your umbrella, it is raining outside!)* | il pleut (impersonal) | Defective/impersonal verb: only conjugated with 'il'. |
| **neiger** | To snow | `neh-ZHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=neiger&op=translate) | `Impersonal Verb` | Il neige abondamment en montagne. *(It is snowing heavily in the mountains.)* | il neige (impersonal) | Impersonal verb. Uses 'il' as dummy subject. |
| **briller** | To shine | `bree-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=briller&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Le soleil brille toute la journée. *(The sun shines all day.)* | je brille, tu brilles, il brille, nous brillons, vous brillez, ils brillent | Liquid 'll' pronounced [j]: [bʁije]. |
| **souffler** | To blow (wind) | `soo-FLAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=souffler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Le mistral souffle fort dans la vallée. *(The mistral wind blows hard in the valley.)* | je souffle, tu souffles, il souffle, nous soufflons, vous soufflez, ils soufflent | Regular -er verb [sufle]. |
| **le ciel** | Sky | `luh SYEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20ciel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le ciel est totalement bleu et dégagé. *(The sky is completely blue and clear.)* | Irregular plural: les cieux [sjø] | Irregular plural: le ciel -> les cieux. |
| **la température** | Temperature | `lah tahn-pay-rah-TYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20temp%C3%A9rature&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La température baisse de cinq degrés. *(The temperature drops by five degrees.)* | les températures | Feminine noun [tɑ̃peʁatyʁ]. |
| **le degré** | Degree | `luh duh-GRAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20degr%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il fait vingt degrés Celsius cet après-midi. *(It is 20 degrees Celsius this afternoon.)* | les degrés | Masculine noun [dəɡʁe]. |
| **la chaleur** | Heat / Warmth | `lah shah-LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20chaleur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La chaleur est intense en plein mois de juillet. *(The heat is intense in the middle of July.)* | les chaleurs | Nouns ending in -eur are usually feminine in French. |
| **le froid** | Cold / Coldness | `luh FRWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20froid&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je n'aime pas le froid de l'hiver. *(I don't like the cold of winter.)* | les froids | Silent final 'd' [fʁwa]. Collocation: 'Il fait froid'. |
| **le parapluie** | Umbrella | `luh pah-rah-PLWEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20parapluie&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | N'oublie pas ton parapluie, il va pleuvoir. *(Don't forget your umbrella, it is going to rain.)* | les parapluies | Masculine noun [paʁaplɥi]. |
| **le manteau** | Coat | `luh mahn-TOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20manteau&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mets ton manteau chaud avant de sortir. *(Put on your warm coat before going out.)* | Irregular plural: les manteaux (takes -x) | Words in -eau take -x in plural: les manteaux. |
| **l'imperméable** | Raincoat | `la(n)-pair-may-AH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27imperm%C3%A9able&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon imperméable me protège bien de l'averse. *(My raincoat protects me well from the downpour.)* | les imperméables | Masculine ending in -e. Elision: l'imperméable. |
| **l'arc-en-ciel** | Rainbow | `lahrk-ahn-SYEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27arc-en-ciel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Regarde le bel arc-en-ciel après l'orage ! *(Look at the beautiful rainbow after the storm!)* | Irregular plural: les arcs-en-ciel | Compound noun. Plural: only 'arc' takes 's' (arcs-en-ciel). |
| **geler** | To freeze | `zhuh-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=geler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | L'eau du lac gèle en plein hiver. *(The lake water freezes in midwinter.)* | je gèle, tu gèles, il gèle, nous gelons, vous gelez, ils gèlent | Stem change: 'e' becomes 'è' before silent endings (il gèle). |

## 7. Immediate Family Members (La famille proche)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la famille** | Family | `lah fah-MEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20famille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma famille habite dans le sud de la France. *(My family lives in the south of France.)* | les familles | Liquid 'll' [famij]. Feminine noun. |
| **les parents** | Parents / Relatives | `lay pah-RAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20parents&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Mes parents sont à la retraite. *(My parents are retired.)* | le parent / les parents | False friend: 'parents' means parents AND extended relatives. |
| **le père** | Father | `luh PAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20p%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon père travaille comme ingénieur. *(My father works as an engineer.)* | les pères | Grave accent: [pɛʁ]. Colloquial: 'papa'. |
| **la mère** | Mother | `lah MAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20m%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma mère est professeur de musique. *(My mother is a music teacher.)* | les mères | Grave accent: [mɛʁ]. Colloquial: 'maman'. |
| **le fils** | Son | `luh FEES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fils&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Leur fils aîné fait des études de droit. *(Their eldest son is studying law.)* | les fils (invariable) | Pronunciation exception: 'l' is silent, 's' IS pronounced: [fis]! |
| **la fille** | Daughter / Girl | `lah FEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20fille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma fille adore dessiner des animaux. *(My daughter loves drawing animals.)* | les filles | Liquid 'll' [fij]. Means daughter or girl. |
| **le frère** | Brother | `luh FRAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fr%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'ai un frère cadet qui a vingt ans. *(I have a younger brother who is twenty.)* | les frères | Grave accent: [fʁɛʁ]. |
| **la sœur** | Sister | `lah SUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20s%C5%93ur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma sœur aînée habite à Montréal. *(My older sister lives in Montreal.)* | les sœurs | Ligature 'œu' pronounced [sœʁ]. |
| **le grand-père** | Grandfather | `luh grahn-PAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20grand-p%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon grand-père me raconte de belles histoires. *(My grandfather tells me great stories.)* | les grands-pères | Silent 'd' in grand [ɡʁɑ̃pɛʁ]. Colloquial: 'papy'. |
| **la grand-mère** | Grandmother | `lah grahn-MAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20grand-m%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma grand-mère cuisine les meilleures tartes. *(My grandmother bakes the best pies.)* | les grand-mères | Note: traditional spelling 'grand-mère' does not add 'e' to grand. |
| **les grands-parents** | Grandparents | `lay grahn-pah-RAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20grands-parents&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Nous rendons visite à nos grands-parents le dimanche. *(We visit our grandparents on Sundays.)* | Masculine plural | Mandatory [z] liaison: nos‿grands-parents (no, but nos‿amis). |
| **le petit-fils** | Grandson | `luh puh-tee-FEES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20petit-fils&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Leur petit-fils vient d'avoir cinq ans. *(Their grandson just turned five.)* | les petits-fils | The 's' in 'fils' is pronounced [pətifis]. |
| **la petite-fille** | Granddaughter | `lah puh-teet-FEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20petite-fille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Leur petite-fille joue du piano. *(Their granddaughter plays the piano.)* | les petites-filles | Feminine: petite sounds the 't': [pətitfij]. |
| **l'oncle** | Uncle | `LOHN-kluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27oncle&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon oncle est vétérinaire en Normandie. *(My uncle is a vet in Normandy.)* | les oncles [lay-ZOHN-kluh] | Masculine. Elision: l'oncle. Plural liaison [z]. |
| **la tante** | Aunt | `lah TAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tante&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma tante nous invite à déjeuner ce dimanche. *(My aunt invites us to lunch this Sunday.)* | les tantes | Nasal vowel [tɑ̃t]. |
| **le cousin** | Cousin (male) | `luh koo-ZA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cousin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon cousin habite dans la même rue que moi. *(My cousin lives on the same street as me.)* | les cousins | Voiced [z] sound between vowels, nasal [kuzɛ̃]. |
| **la cousine** | Cousin (female) | `lah koo-ZEEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cousine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma cousine prépare un voyage en Italie. *(My cousin is preparing a trip to Italy.)* | les cousines | Oral vowel [kuzin]. Distinct from masculine nasal [kuzɛ̃]. |
| **le neveu** | Nephew | `luh nuh-VUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20neveu&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon neveu apprend à faire du vélo. *(My nephew is learning to ride a bike.)* | Irregular plural: les neveux (takes -x) | Nouns in -eu take -x in plural: les neveux. |
| **la nièce** | Niece | `lah NYES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20ni%C3%A8ce&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma nièce fête son dixième anniversaire. *(My niece celebrates her tenth birthday.)* | les nièces | Grave accent: [njɛs]. |
| **le mari** | Husband | `luh mah-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mari&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Son mari est architecte à Lyon. *(Her husband is an architect in Lyon.)* | les maris | Masculine noun [maʁi]. |
| **l'époux / l'épouse** | Spouse (husband / wife) | `lay-POO / lay-POOZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9poux%20/%20l%27%C3%A9pouse&op=translate) | `Masculine / Feminine` | Je vous présente mon épouse. *(May I introduce my wife.)* | les époux / les épouses | Masculine silent 'x' [epu]; feminine voiced 's' [epuz]. |
| **le beau-père** | Father-in-law / Stepfather | `luh boh-PAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20beau-p%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon beau-père est très passionné d'histoire. *(My father-in-law is very passionate about history.)* | les beaux-pères | Compound plural: both parts take -s/-x: les beaux-pères. |
| **la belle-mère** | Mother-in-law / Stepmother | `lah bel-MAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20belle-m%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma belle-mère nous aide avec les enfants. *(My mother-in-law helps us with the children.)* | les belles-mères | Compound plural: les belles-mères. |
| **le beau-frère** | Brother-in-law / Stepbrother | `luh boh-FRAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20beau-fr%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mon beau-frère joue au tennis avec moi. *(My brother-in-law plays tennis with me.)* | les beaux-frères | Plural: les beaux-frères. |
| **la belle-sœur** | Sister-in-law / Stepsister | `lah bel-SUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20belle-s%C5%93ur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma belle-sœur travaille à l'hôpital. *(My sister-in-law works at the hospital.)* | les belles-sœurs | Plural: les belles-sœurs. |
| **le bébé** | Baby | `luh bay-BAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20b%C3%A9b%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le bébé dort paisiblement dans son berceau. *(The baby sleeps peacefully in his crib.)* | les bébés | Grammatically masculine even for a baby girl. |
| **l'aîné / l'aînée** | Eldest child | `lay-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27a%C3%AEn%C3%A9%20/%20l%27a%C3%AEn%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Noun / Adjective (m./f.)` | Thomas est l'aîné de trois enfants. *(Thomas is the eldest of three children.)* | les aînés / les aînées | Elision: l'aîné. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **le cadet / la cadette** | Youngest child | `luh kah-DEH / lah kah-DET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cadet%20/%20la%20cadette&op=translate) | `Noun / Adjective (m./f.)` | Ma cadette commence l'école maternelle. *(My youngest daughter starts nursery school.)* | les cadets / les cadettes | Masculine silent 't' [kadɛ]; feminine doubled 'tt' sounds [kadɛt]. |
| **les jumeaux / les jumelles** | Twins (m. / f.) | `lay zhyoo-MOH / lay zhyoo-MEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20jumeaux%20/%20les%20jumelles&op=translate) | `Plural (m. pl. / f. pl.)` | Elle a donné naissance à des jumeaux. *(She gave birth to twins.)* | un jumeau -> des jumeaux / une jumelle -> des jumelles | Irregular plural: jumeau takes -x; jumelle takes -s. |
| **élever** | To raise (children) | `ay-luh-VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9lever&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Mes parents ont élevé quatre enfants avec amour. *(My parents raised four children with love.)* | j'élève, tu élèves, il élève, nous élevons, vous élevez, ils élèvent | Stem change: 'e' becomes 'è' before silent endings (j'élève). |

## 8. Colors (Les couleurs)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la couleur** | Color | `lah koo-LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20couleur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le bleu est ma couleur préférée. *(Blue is my favorite color.)* | les couleurs | Feminine noun [kuluʁ]. All specific colors used as nouns are masculine (le rouge). |
| **blanc / blanche** | White | `BLAHN / BLAHNSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=blanc%20/%20blanche&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La maison a des volets blancs. *(The house has white shutters.)* | blancs / blanches | Masculine silent 'c' [blɑ̃]; feminine adds -he [blɑ̃ʃ]. |
| **noir / noire** | Black | `NWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=noir%20/%20noire&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il porte une veste noire et élégante. *(He wears a black, elegant jacket.)* | noirs / noires | Pronunciation identical for both genders: [nwaʁ]. |
| **rouge** | Red | `ROOZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=rouge&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle conduit une petite voiture rouge. *(She drives a small red car.)* | rouges | Epicene adjective: identical spelling and pronunciation m./f. |
| **bleu / bleue** | Blue | `BLUH [blø]` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bleu%20/%20bleue&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le ciel est d'un bleu éclatant. *(The sky is a brilliant blue.)* | bleus / bleues | Regular plural: takes -s (bleus), not -x! Feminine adds '-e'. |
| **jaune** | Yellow | `ZHOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=jaune&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le tournesol est une fleur jaune. *(The sunflower is a yellow flower.)* | jaunes | Epicene adjective ending in -e. |
| **vert / verte** | Green | `VAIR / VAIRT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vert%20/%20verte&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'herbe du jardin est bien verte. *(The garden grass is very green.)* | verts / vertes | Masculine silent 't' [vɛʁ]; feminine sounds 't' [vɛʁt]. |
| **gris / grise** | Gray | `GREE / GREEZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gris%20/%20grise&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le ciel est gris et nuageux ce matin. *(The sky is gray and cloudy this morning.)* | gris (invariable) / grises | Masculine ends in silent 's' (invariable in plural); feminine [ɡʁiz]. |
| **marron** | Brown / Chestnut | `mah-ROHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=marron&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | Elle porte des chaussures marron. *(She wears brown shoes.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Grammar rule: colors derived from fruit/nuts are invariable (des yeux marron)! |
| **orange** | Orange | `oh-RAHNZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=orange&op=translate) | `Adjective (invariable)` | J'ai acheté deux t-shirts orange. *(I bought two orange t-shirts.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Grammar rule: derived from the fruit, so it is strictly invariable! |
| **rose** | Pink / Rose | `ROHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=rose&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle a choisi une belle robe rose. *(She chose a pretty pink dress.)* | roses (takes -s) | Exception to fruit/flower rule: 'rose' DOES agree in plural (des robes roses). |
| **violet / violette** | Purple / Violet | `vyoh-LEH / vyoh-LET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=violet%20/%20violette&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette fleur violette dégage un doux parfum. *(This purple flower gives off a sweet scent.)* | violets / violettes | Feminine doubles 't': violette [vjɔlɛt]. |
| **brun / brune** | Dark-haired / Brown | `BRUH(N) / BROON` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=brun%20/%20brune&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon frère est grand et brun. *(My brother is tall and dark-haired.)* | bruns / brunes | Used for hair/eyes/skin. Masculine nasal [bʁœ̃]; feminine [bʁyn]. |
| **blond / blonde** | Blonde / Fair-haired | `BLOHN / BLOHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=blond%20/%20blonde&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Sa fille a de magnifiques cheveux blonds. *(Her daughter has magnificent blonde hair.)* | blonds / blondes | Masculine silent 'd' [blɔ̃]; feminine sounds 'd' [blɔ̃d]. |
| **roux / rousse** | Red-haired / Auburn | `ROO / ROOS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=roux%20/%20rousse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cet enfant a les cheveux roux. *(This child has red hair.)* | roux (invariable) / rousses | Irregular feminine: roux -> rousse [ʁus]. Masculine plural invariable. |
| **clair / claire** | Light (color) / Clear | `KLAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=clair%20/%20claire&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il porte une chemise bleu clair. *(He wears a light blue shirt.)* | clairs / claires | Compound color rule: compound colors are INVARIABLE (des yeux bleu clair). |
| **foncé / foncée** | Dark (color) | `fohn-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fonc%C3%A9%20/%20fonc%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | J'ai commandé un costume gris foncé. *(I ordered a dark gray suit.)* | foncés / foncées | In compound colors, remains invariable: des chemises vert foncé. |
| **vif / vive** | Vivid / Bright | `VEEF / VEEV` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vif%20/%20vive&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle aime les couleurs vives comme le rouge. *(She loves bright colors like red.)* | vifs / vives | Pattern -f -> -ve: vif [vif] -> vive [viv]. |
| **pâle** | Pale | `PAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=p%C3%A2le&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le malade a le teint très pâle. *(The patient has a very pale complexion.)* | pâles | Circumflex on 'â' [pal]. Epicene adjective. |
| **doré / dorée** | Golden / Gilded | `doh-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dor%C3%A9%20/%20dor%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le cadre doré met en valeur le tableau. *(The golden frame enhances the painting.)* | dorés / dorées | Derived from l'or (gold). Regular agreement. |
| **argenté / argentée** | Silver-colored | `ar-zhahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=argent%C3%A9%20/%20argent%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle a acheté des boucles d'oreilles argentées. *(She bought silver-colored earrings.)* | argentés / argentées | Derived from l'argent (silver). |
| **sombre** | Dark / Gloomy | `SOHN-bruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sombre&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La pièce est sombre, allume la lumière. *(The room is dark, turn on the light.)* | sombres | Epicene adjective ending in -e. |
| **brillant / brillante** | Shiny / Brilliant | `bree-YAHN / bree-YAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=brillant%20/%20brillante&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le sol brille avec ce vernis brillant. *(The floor shines with this shiny polish.)* | brillants / brillantes | Liquid 'll' [bʁijɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [bʁijɑ̃t]. |
| **mat / mate** | Matte / Non-glossy | `MAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mat%20/%20mate&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je préfère une finition mate pour les murs. *(I prefer a matte finish for walls.)* | mats / mates | Final 't' is sounded in both genders: [mat]. |
| **coloré / colorée** | Colorful | `koh-loh-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=color%C3%A9%20/%20color%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce marché provençal est très coloré. *(This Provencal market is very colorful.)* | colorés / colorées | Regular adjective [kɔlɔʁe]. |
| **multicolore** | Multicolored | `mool-tee-koh-LOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=multicolore&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Les enfants adorent ce cerf-volant multicolore. *(Children love this multicolored kite.)* | multicolores | Epicene adjective ending in -e. |
| **teinter** | To tint / To dye | `ta(n)-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=teinter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | L'artisan teinte le bois en chêne foncé. *(The craftsman tints the wood in dark oak.)* | je teinte, tu teintes, il teinte, nous teintons, vous teintez, ils teintent | Regular -er verb [tɛ̃te]. |
| **peindre** | To paint | `PA(N)-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=peindre&op=translate) | `Verb (3rd group)` | Nous peignons les murs de la chambre en bleu. *(We are painting the bedroom walls blue.)* | je peins, tu peins, il peint, nous peignons, vous peignez, ils peignent | Stem change to peign- in plural forms [pɛɲɔ̃]. |
| **la peinture** | Paint / Painting | `lah pa(n)-TYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20peinture&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La peinture à l'huile sèche lentement. *(Oil paint dries slowly.)* | les peintures | Nouns in -ure are feminine. |
| **le pinceau** | Paintbrush | `luh pa(n)-SOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pinceau&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nettoie bien tes pinceaux après usage. *(Clean your paintbrushes well after use.)* | Irregular plural: les pinceaux (takes -x) | Words in -eau take -x in plural: les pinceaux. |
