## 9. Nationalities and Countries (Les nationalités et les pays)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la France** | France | `lah FRAHNSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20France&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | J'habite en France depuis deux ans. *(I have lived in France for two years.)* | Preposition: en France | Feminine countries take 'en': en France, en Espagne, en Italie. |
| **français / française** | French (m./f.) | `frahn-SEH / frahn-SEHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fran%C3%A7ais%20/%20fran%C3%A7aise&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon ami est français et parle trois langues. *(My friend is French and speaks three languages.)* | français (invariable) / françaises | Nationality adjectives are lowercase in French (un livre français); capitalized only as nouns (les Français). |
| **l'Angleterre (f.)** | England | `lahn-gluh-TAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Angleterre%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Londres est la capitale de l'Angleterre. *(London is the capital of England.)* | Preposition: en Angleterre | Elision l' + vowel. Takes 'en' [ɑ̃.n‿ɑ̃ɡlətɛʁ] with [n] liaison. |
| **anglais / anglaise** | English (m./f.) | `ahn-GLEH / ahn-GLEHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=anglais%20/%20anglaise&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle étudie la littérature anglaise. *(She studies English literature.)* | anglais (invariable) / anglaises | Masculine ends in 's', invariable in plural: des livres anglais. |
| **l'Espagne (f.)** | Spain | `les-PAH-nyuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Espagne%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Nous partons en vacances en Espagne. *(We are going on holiday to Spain.)* | Preposition: en Espagne | Liaison: en‿Espagne [ɑ̃.n‿ɛspaɲ]. Palatal gn [ɲ]. |
| **espagnol / espagnole** | Spanish (m./f.) | `es-pah-NYOHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=espagnol%20/%20espagnole&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Il adore la cuisine espagnole. *(He loves Spanish cuisine.)* | espagnols / espagnoles | Pronunciation identical m./f. [ɛspaɲɔl]. Regular plural takes -s. |
| **l'Italie (f.)** | Italy | `lee-tah-LEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Italie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Rome est une ville magnifique en Italie. *(Rome is a magnificent city in Italy.)* | Preposition: en Italie | Takes 'en': en Italie [ɑ̃.n‿itali]. |
| **italien / italienne** | Italian (m./f.) | `ee-tah-LYA(N) / ee-tah-LYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=italien%20/%20italienne&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce restaurant italien prépare d'excellentes pâtes. *(This Italian restaurant prepares excellent pasta.)* | italiens / italiennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n' and opens vowel to [jɛn]. |
| **l'Allemagne (f.)** | Germany | `lahl-MAH-nyuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Allemagne%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Berlin se trouve en Allemagne. *(Berlin is located in Germany.)* | Preposition: en Allemagne | Feminine country. Preposition: en Allemagne [ɑ̃.n‿almaɲ]. |
| **allemand / allemande** | German (m./f.) | `ahl-MAHN / ahl-MAHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=allemand%20/%20allemande&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon professeur d'allemand est très gentil. *(My German teacher is very kind.)* | allemands / allemandes | Masculine silent 'd' [almɑ̃]; feminine sounds 'd' [almɑ̃d]. |
| **le Canada** | Canada | `luh kah-nah-DAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Canada&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m.)` | Mon oncle habite au Canada depuis dix ans. *(My uncle has lived in Canada for ten years.)* | Preposition: au Canada (à + le = au) | Masculine countries take 'au' (au Canada, au Japon, au Mexique). |
| **canadien / canadienne** | Canadian (m./f.) | `kah-nah-DYA(N) / kah-nah-DYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=canadien%20/%20canadienne&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | L'équipe canadienne de hockey est réputée. *(The Canadian hockey team is renowned.)* | canadiens / canadiennes | Doubles 'n' in feminine: canadienne [kanadjɛn]. |
| **les États-Unis (m. pl.)** | United States | `lay zay-tah-zoo-NEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20%C3%89tats-Unis%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Plural Country (m. pl.)` | Elle étudie aux États-Unis cette année. *(She is studying in the United States this year.)* | Preposition: aux États-Unis (à + les = aux) | Mandatory double liaison: aux‿États-Unis [o-z-eta-z-yni] with [z]! |
| **américain / américaine** | American (m./f.) | `ah-may-ree-KA(N) / ah-may-ree-KEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=am%C3%A9ricain%20/%20am%C3%A9ricaine&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est un film américain très populaire. *(It's a very popular American movie.)* | américains / américaines | Masculine nasal [amøʁikɛ̃]; feminine oral [amøʁikɛn]. |
| **la Suisse** | Switzerland | `lah SWEES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20Suisse&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Genève est une grande ville de Suisse. *(Geneva is a large city in Switzerland.)* | Preposition: en Suisse | Feminine country. Preposition: en Suisse [ɑ̃ sɥis]. |
| **suisse** | Swiss (m./f.) | `SWEES` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=suisse&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Le chocolat suisse est délicieux. *(Swiss chocolate is delicious.)* | suisses | Epicene adjective: identical spelling and pronunciation in m. and f. |
| **la Belgique** | Belgium | `lah bel-ZHEEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20Belgique&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | Bruxelles est située en Belgique. *(Brussels is located in Belgium.)* | Preposition: en Belgique | Feminine country. Preposition: en Belgique. |
| **belge** | Belgian (m./f.) | `BELZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=belge&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Nous adorons les gaufres belges. *(We love Belgian waffles.)* | belges | Epicene adjective ending in -e. |
| **le Portugal** | Portugal | `luh por-tyoo-GAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Portugal&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m.)` | Lisbonne est la capitale du Portugal. *(Lisbon is the capital of Portugal.)* | Preposition: au Portugal | Masculine country: au Portugal (going to / in), du Portugal (from). |
| **portugais / portugaise** | Portuguese (m./f.) | `por-tyoo-GEH / por-tyoo-GEHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=portugais%20/%20portugaise&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle parle couramment le portugais. *(She speaks Portuguese fluently.)* | portugais (invariable) / portugaises | Masculine silent 's' [pɔʁtyɡɛ]; feminine sounds 'z' [pɔʁtyɡɛz]. |
| **le Japon** | Japan | `luh zhah-POHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20Japon&op=translate) | `Masculine Country (m.)` | Tokyo se trouve au Japon. *(Tokyo is in Japan.)* | Preposition: au Japon | Masculine country. Preposition: au Japon. |
| **japonais / japonaise** | Japanese (m./f.) | `zhah-poh-NEH / zhah-poh-NEHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=japonais%20/%20japonaise&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | J'aime beaucoup la nourriture japonaise. *(I really like Japanese food.)* | japonais (invariable) / japonaises | Masculine plural invariable: des mangas japonais. |
| **la Chine** | China | `lah SHEEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20Chine&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | La Grande Muraille se trouve en Chine. *(The Great Wall is in China.)* | Preposition: en Chine | Feminine country. Preposition: en Chine. |
| **chinois / chinoise** | Chinese (m./f.) | `shee-NWAH / shee-NWAHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=chinois%20/%20chinoise&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Il apprend la langue chinoise à l'université. *(He is learning Chinese at university.)* | chinois (invariable) / chinoises | Masculine plural invariable: des plats chinois. |
| **l'Inde (f.)** | India | `la(n)d` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Inde%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Country (f.)` | New Delhi est la capitale de l'Inde. *(New Delhi is the capital of India.)* | Preposition: en Inde | Feminine country. Preposition: en Inde [ɑ̃.n‿ɛ̃d]. |
| **indien / indienne** | Indian (m./f.) | `a(n)-DYA(N) / a(n)-DYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=indien%20/%20indienne&op=translate) | `Nationality / Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce restaurant indien sert d'excellents plats. *(This Indian restaurant serves excellent dishes.)* | indiens / indiennes | Doubles 'n' in feminine: indienne [ɛ̃djɛn]. |
| **le pays** | Country / Land | `luh pay-EE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pays&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel pays souhaitez-vous visiter ? *(Which country would you like to visit?)* | les pays (invariable in plural) | Masculine noun ending in 's', identical in singular and plural: un pays, des pays. |
| **la nationalité** | Nationality | `lah nah-syoh-nah-lee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20nationalit%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle est votre nationalité ? *(What is your nationality?)* | les nationalités | Nouns ending in -ité are almost always feminine. |
| **habiter** | To live / To reside | `ah-bee-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=habiter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | J'habite dans un bel appartement au centre. *(I live in a beautiful apartment downtown.)* | j'habite, tu habites, il habite, nous habitons, vous habitez, ils habitent | Silent 'h' (h muet): elision j'habite and [z] liaison vous‿habitez [vuzabite]. |
| **venir** | To come | `vuh-NEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=venir&op=translate) | `Verb (3rd group)` | Je viens du Maroc et lui vient du Sénégal. *(I come from Morocco and he comes from Senegal.)* | je viens, tu viens, il vient, nous venons, vous venez, ils viennent | Stem changes: viens / venons / viennent. Preposition for origin: venir de. |

## 10. Common Professions and Occupations (Les professions)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la profession** | Profession / Occupation | `lah proh-feh-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20profession&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle profession exercez-vous ? *(What profession do you practice?)* | les professions | Nouns ending in -sion / -tion are feminine [fɛsjɔ̃]. |
| **le métier** | Trade / Job / Craft | `luh may-TYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20m%C3%A9tier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | C'est un métier passionnant et exigeant. *(It is an exciting and demanding trade.)* | les métiers | Masculine noun [metje]. Final 'r' is silent. |
| **le professeur / la professeure** | Teacher / Professor | `luh proh-feh-SUHR / lah proh-feh-SUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20professeur%20/%20la%20professeure&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Elle est professeure de mathématiques. *(She is a mathematics teacher.)* | les professeurs / les professeures | Grammar rule: no indefinite article after être with professions (Elle est professeur). |
| **le médecin** | Doctor / Physician | `luh mayd-SA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20m%C3%A9decin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le médecin ausculte le patient avec soin. *(The doctor examines the patient carefully.)* | les médecins | Typically masculine even when referring to a woman: Elle est médecin. |
| **l'infirmier (m.) / l'infirmière (f.)** | Nurse | `la(n)-feer-MYAY / la(n)-feer-MYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27infirmier%20%28m.%29%20/%20l%27infirmi%C3%A8re%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | L'infirmière s'occupe des patients avec douceur. *(The nurse takes care of the patients gently.)* | les infirmiers / les infirmières | Masculine silent 'r' [ɛ̃fiʁmje]; feminine pronounced [ɛ̃fiʁmjɛʁ]. |
| **l'ingénieur (m.)** | Engineer | `la(n)-zhay-NYUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ing%C3%A9nieur%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Mon frère est ingénieur en informatique. *(My brother is a computer engineer.)* | les ingénieurs | Modern feminine form: l'ingénieure. Liaison: un‿ingénieur [œ̃.n‿ɛ̃ʒenjœʁ]. |
| **l'avocat (m.) / l'avocate (f.)** | Lawyer / Attorney | `lah-voh-KAH / lah-voh-KAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27avocat%20%28m.%29%20/%20l%27avocate%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | L'avocate défend son client devant le tribunal. *(The lawyer defends her client in court.)* | les avocats / les avocates | Masculine silent 't' [avɔka]; feminine sounds 't' [avɔkat]. Note: un avocat also means 'avocado'! |
| **le policier / la policière** | Police officer | `luh poh-lee-SYAY / lah poh-lee-SYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20policier%20/%20la%20polici%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Le policier régule la circulation au carrefour. *(The police officer directs traffic at the intersection.)* | les policiers / les policières | Masculine [pɔlisje]; feminine [pɔlisjɛʁ]. |
| **le pompier** | Firefighter | `luh pohn-PYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pompier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les pompiers arrivent rapidement sur les lieux. *(The firefighters arrive quickly on the scene.)* | les pompiers | Nasal [pɔ̃pje]. Silent final 'r'. |
| **le cuisinier / la cuisinière** | Cook / Chef | `luh kwee-zee-NYAY / lah kwee-zee-NYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cuisinier%20/%20la%20cuisini%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Ce chef cuisinier prépare de savoureux repas. *(This chef prepares delicious meals.)* | les cuisiniers / les cuisinières | False friend: 'la cuisinière' also means the stove / cooker! |
| **le serveur / la serveuse** | Waiter / Waitress | `luh sair-VUHR / lah sair-VUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20serveur%20/%20la%20serveuse&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Le serveur nous apporte la carte des desserts. *(The waiter brings us the dessert menu.)* | les serveurs / les serveuses | Pattern -eur -> -euse: serveur -> serveuse [sɛʁvøz]. |
| **le boulanger / la boulangère** | Baker | `luh boo-lahn-ZHAY / lah boo-lahn-ZHAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20boulanger%20/%20la%20boulang%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Le boulanger cuit du pain frais chaque matin. *(The baker bakes fresh bread every morning.)* | les boulangers / les boulangères | The bakery shop is feminine: la boulangerie. |
| **le boucher / la bouchère** | Butcher | `luh boo-SHAY / lah boo-SHAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20boucher%20/%20la%20bouch%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | La bouchère conseille de la viande de qualité. *(The butcher recommends high-quality meat.)* | les bouchers / les bouchères | The shop is la boucherie. |
| **le pharmacien / la pharmacienne** | Pharmacist | `luh far-mah-SYA(N) / lah far-mah-SYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pharmacien%20/%20la%20pharmacienne&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Le pharmacien me donne les médicaments prescrits. *(The pharmacist gives me the prescribed medicines.)* | les pharmaciens / les pharmaciennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n'. |
| **le dentiste / la dentiste** | Dentist | `luh dahn-TEEST / lah dahn-TEEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dentiste%20/%20la%20dentiste&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Je vais chez le dentiste pour un contrôle annuel. *(I am going to the dentist for an annual check-up.)* | les dentistes | Epicene noun: identical in masculine and feminine (le/la dentiste). Preposition: chez le dentiste (not à). |
| **le journaliste / la journaliste** | Journalist | `luh zhoor-nah-LEEST / lah zhoor-nah-LEEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20journaliste%20/%20la%20journaliste&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Cette journaliste écrit des articles captivants. *(This journalist writes captivating articles.)* | les journalistes | Epicene ending in -iste. |
| **l'architecte (m./f.)** | Architect | `lar-shee-TEKT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27architecte%20%28m./f.%29&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | L'architecte dessine les plans d'un nouvel immeuble. *(The architect draws the plans of a new building.)* | les architectes | Pronounced with 'ch' = [ʃ] [aʁʃitɛkt]. Epicene. |

Epicene. |

[elɛktʁisjɛ̃]. |

In French, applies to bus, taxi, or truck drivers. |
| **le vendeur / la vendeuse** | Salesperson / Shop assistant | `luh vahn-DUHR / lah vahn-DUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vendeur%20/%20la%20vendeuse&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | La vendeuse conseille les clients avec le sourire. *(The salesperson advises customers with a smile.)* | les vendeurs / les vendeuses | From the verb vendre (to sell). |
| **l'étudiant (m.) / l'étudiante (f.)** | Student (university) | `lay-tyoo-DYA(N) / lay-tyoo-DYAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9tudiant%20%28m.%29%20/%20l%27%C3%A9tudiante%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Les étudiants révisent à la bibliothèque. *(The students are studying at the library.)* | les étudiants / les étudiantes | Masculine silent 't' [etydjɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [etydjɑ̃t]. |
| **travailler** | To work | `trah-vah-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=travailler&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Je travaille du lundi au vendredi. *(I work from Monday to Friday.)* | je travaille, tu travailles, il travaille, nous travaillons, vous travaillez, ils travaillent | Liquid -ill- sounds like 'y' [tʁavaje]. Regular conjugation. |
| **chercher un emploi** | To look for a job | `shair-SHAY œ̃-nahn-PLWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=chercher%20un%20emploi&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb` | Après ses études, il cherche un emploi stable. *(After his studies, he is looking for a steady job.)* | je cherche un emploi, nous cherchons un emploi | Liaison: un‿emploi [œ̃.n‿ɑ̃plwa] with [n]. |

## 11. Parts of the Body (Les parties du corps)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le corps** | Body | `luh KOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20corps&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le sport est bénéfique pour le corps et l'esprit. *(Sports are beneficial for the body and mind.)* | les corps (invariable in plural) | Silent 'ps' [kɔʁ]. Invariable in plural because it ends in 's'. |
| **la tête** | Head | `lah TET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20t%C3%AAte&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai mal à la tête aujourd'hui. *(I have a headache today.)* | les têtes | Circumflex accent on 'ê' [tɛt]. Avoir mal à la tête = to have a headache. |
| **le visage** | Face | `luh vee-ZAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20visage&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle a un visage souriant et lumineux. *(She has a smiling and bright face.)* | les visages | Nouns in -age are masculine (le visage). |
| **l'œil (m.) / les yeux (m. pl.)** | Eye / Eyes | `LUY / lay ZYUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C5%93il%20%28m.%29%20/%20les%20yeux%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Irregular Noun (m.)` | Elle a de grands yeux bleus. *(She has big blue eyes.)* | Irregular plural: un œil -> les yeux | Extreme irregular plural! Mandatory liaison: les‿yeux [le-z-jø] with [z]. |
| **le nez** | Nose | `luh NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20nez&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il a le nez qui coule à cause du rhume. *(He has a runny nose because of a cold.)* | les nez (invariable in plural) | Silent 'z' [ne]. Ends in 'z', so invariable in plural: un nez, des nez. |
| **la bouche** | Mouth | `lah BOOSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bouche&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ouvre la bouche pour prendre le sirop. *(Open your mouth to take the syrup.)* | les bouches | Pronounced [buʃ]. |
| **les dents (f. pl.)** | Teeth | `lay DAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20dents%20%28f.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Plural (f. pl.)` | Il faut se brosser les dents deux fois par jour. *(You should brush your teeth twice a day.)* | une dent (f.) -> les dents | Silent final 't' and 's' [dɑ̃]. Se brosser les dents = reflexive. |
| **la langue** | Tongue / Language | `lah LAHNG` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20langue&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le médecin regarde la langue du malade. *(The doctor examines the patient's tongue.)* | les langues | Dual meaning: the anatomical tongue and a spoken language. |
| **l'oreille (f.)** | Ear | `loh-REH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27oreille%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai mal à l'oreille gauche depuis hier. *(My left ear has been hurting since yesterday.)* | les oreilles | Liquid -eille [ɔʁɛj]. Liaison: les‿oreilles [le-z-ɔʁɛj]. |
| **les cheveux (m. pl.)** | Hair | `lay shuh-VUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Elle a les cheveux châtains et ondulés. *(She has brown, wavy hair.)* | un cheveu -> les cheveux (takes -x) | In French, hair is always plural (les cheveux). Singular un cheveu = a single strand. |
| **le cou** | Neck | `luh KOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cou&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il porte une écharpe chaude autour du cou. *(He wears a warm scarf around his neck.)* | les cous | Pronounced [ku]. Takes regular -s in plural: les cous. |
| **la gorge** | Throat | `lah GORZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20gorge&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai mal à la gorge en avalant. *(I have a sore throat when swallowing.)* | les gorges | Avoir mal à la gorge = to have a sore throat. |
| **l'épaule (f.)** | Shoulder | `lay-POHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9paule%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il porte son sac de sport sur l'épaule. *(He carries his gym bag on his shoulder.)* | les épaules | Feminine noun [epol]. Liaison: les‿épaules [le-z-epol]. |
| **le bras** | Arm | `luh BRAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20bras&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il s'est cassé le bras en jouant au basket. *(He broke his arm playing basketball.)* | les bras (invariable in plural) | Silent 's' [bʁa]. Invariable in plural: un bras, deux bras. |
| **le coude** | Elbow | `luh KOOD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20coude&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ne mets pas tes coudes sur la table ! *(Don't put your elbows on the table!)* | les coudes | Masculine noun [kud]. |
| **la main** | Hand | `lah MA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20main&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Donne-moi la main pour traverser la rue. *(Give me your hand to cross the street.)* | les mains | Gender exception! Noun in -in is feminine (la main), unlike le pain, le train. |
| **le doigt** | Finger | `luh DWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20doigt&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il porte une alliance en or au doigt. *(He wears a gold wedding band on his finger.)* | les doigts | Silent 'g' and 't' [dwa]. |

Liaison: les‿ongles [le-z-ɔ̃ɡl]. |

| **le cœur** | Heart | `luh KUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20c%C5%93ur&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Son cœur bat plus vite après la course. *(His heart beats faster after the run.)* | les cœurs | Ligature œ [kœʁ]. Masculine noun. |
| **le dos** | Back | `luh DOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dos&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je porte mon sac à dos sur le dos. *(I carry my backpack on my back.)* | les dos (invariable in plural) | Silent 's' [do]. Invariable in plural: un dos, des dos. |
| **le ventre** | Belly / Stomach (abdomen) | `luh VAHN-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20ventre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | J'ai mal au ventre après ce grand repas. *(I have a stomach ache after this big meal.)* | les ventres | Masculine noun [vɑ̃tʁ]. Avoir mal au ventre = stomach ache. |
| **l'estomac (m.)** | Stomach (organ) | `les-toh-MAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27estomac%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ne prends pas ce médicament l'estomac vide. *(Don't take this medicine on an empty stomach.)* | les estomacs | Silent 'c' [ɛstɔma]. Plural estomacs also silent 'cs'. |

| **le genou** | Knee | `luh zhuh-NOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20genou&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le joueur est blessé au genou droit. *(The player is injured in the right knee.)* | Irregular plural: les genoux (takes -x) | One of 7 nouns ending in -ou that take -x in plural (les genoux)! |
| **la cheville** | Ankle | `lah shuh-VEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cheville&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Elle s'est tordu la cheville en descendant l'escalier. *(She twisted her ankle going down the stairs.)* | les chevilles | Liquid -ille [ʃəvij]. |
| **le pied** | Foot | `luh PYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pied&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous nous déplaçons souvent à pied. *(We often travel on foot.)* | les pieds | Silent 'd' [pje]. Expression: à pied = on foot. |
| **l'orteil (m.)** | Toe | `lor-TEH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27orteil%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il s'est cogné le gros orteil contre le lit. *(He stubbed his big toe against the bed.)* | les orteils | Masculine noun [ɔʁtɛj]. Also called 'le doigt de pied'. |
| **la peau** | Skin | `lah POH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20peau&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Cette crème hydrate bien la peau sèche. *(This cream hydrates dry skin well.)* | les peaux (takes -x) | Gender exception! Noun in -eau is feminine (la peau, l'eau), unlike le manteau. |
| **avoir mal à** | To hurt / To have an ache in | `ah-VWAHR MAHL ah` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20mal%20%C3%A0&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | J'ai mal aux dents et je dois consulter. *(My teeth hurt and I must see a dentist.)* | j'ai mal au..., tu as mal à la..., il a mal aux... | Contraction rule: à + le = au (au dos), à + la = à la (à la tête), à + les = aux (aux yeux). |

## 12. Physical Descriptions of People (La description physique)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **grand / grande** | Tall / Big | `GRAHN / GRAHND` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=grand%20/%20grande&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon frère est très grand pour son âge. *(My brother is very tall for his age.)* | grands / grandes | BANGS adjective: placed BEFORE the noun (un grand homme). Masculine silent 'd' [ɡʁɑ̃]; feminine sounds 'd' [ɡʁɑ̃d]. |
| **petit / petite** | Short / Small | `puh-TEE / puh-TEET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=petit%20/%20petite&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle habite dans un petit village de montagne. *(She lives in a small mountain village.)* | petits / petites | BANGS adjective: placed BEFORE the noun. Masculine silent 't' [pəti]; feminine [pətit]. |
| **mince** | Thin / Slim | `MA(N)SS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mince&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il reste très mince grâce à la course à pied. *(He stays very slim thanks to running.)* | minces | Epicene adjective ending in -e: identical m./f. [mɛ̃s]. |
| **gros / grosse** | Fat / Large / Heavy | `GROH / GROHSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gros%20/%20grosse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il porte une grosse valise noire. *(He carries a big black suitcase.)* | gros (invariable) / grosses | Irregular feminine doubles 'ss': grosse [ɡʁos]. Plural masculine invariable. |
| **fort / forte** | Strong / Heavily built | `FOR / FORT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fort%20/%20forte&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cet ouvrier est très fort physiquement. *(This worker is very strong physically.)* | forts / fortes | Masculine silent 't' [fɔʁ]; feminine sounds 't' [fɔʁt]. Polite euphemism for 'heavy'. |
| **faible** | Weak / Fragile | `FEH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faible&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Après la grippe, le patient se sent encore faible. *(After the flu, the patient still feels weak.)* | faibles | Epicene adjective ending in -e [fɛbl]. |
| **beau / bel / belle** | Handsome / Beautiful | `BOH / BEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=beau%20/%20bel%20/%20belle&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./m. vowel/f.)` | C'est un bel homme et une belle femme. *(He is a handsome man and a beautiful woman.)* | beaux (takes -x) / belles | Special masculine form: 'bel' before a masculine noun starting with vowel/silent h (un bel homme). BANGS adjective. |

Pronounced identically m./f. [ʒɔli]. |
| **laid / laide** | Ugly | `LEH / LED` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=laid%20/%20laide&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce vieux bâtiment gris est vraiment laid. *(This old gray building is really ugly.)* | laids / laides | Masculine silent 'd' [lɛ]; feminine sounds 'd' [lɛd]. |
| **jeune** | Young | `ZHUHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=jeune&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | De jeunes étudiants visitent le musée. *(Young students are visiting the museum.)* | jeunes | BANGS adjective: placed before the noun. Epicene ending in -e [ʒœn]. |
| **vieux / vieil / vieille** | Old / Elderly | `VYUH / VYAY / VYEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vieux%20/%20vieil%20/%20vieille&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./m. vowel/f.)` | Mon vieil ami habite dans cette vieille maison. *(My old friend lives in this old house.)* | vieux (invariable) / vieilles | Special form: 'vieil' before masculine vowel/silent h (un vieil homme). BANGS adjective. |
| **âgé / âgée** | Elderly / Aged | `ah-ZHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A2g%C3%A9%20/%20%C3%A2g%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette dame âgée marche avec une canne. *(This elderly lady walks with a cane.)* | âgés / âgées | More polite and respectful than 'vieux' when describing people. |
| **chauve** | Bald | `SHOHV` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=chauve&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon grand-père est complètement chauve. *(My grandfather is completely bald.)* | chauves | Epicene adjective ending in -e [ʃov]. |
| **barbu / barbue** | Bearded | `bar-BYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=barbu%20/%20barbue&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'homme barbu assis près de la fenêtre lit le journal. *(The bearded man sitting near the window reads the newspaper.)* | barbus / barbues | From the noun la barbe [baʁby]. |
| **la barbe** | Beard | `lah BARB` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20barbe&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il taille sa barbe tous les deux jours. *(He trims his beard every two days.)* | les barbes | Feminine noun [baʁb]. |
| **la moustache** | Moustache | `lah moos-TAHSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20moustache&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il a une fine moustache bien soignée. *(He has a well-groomed, fine moustache.)* | les moustaches | Feminine noun [mustaʃ]. |
| **les lunettes (f. pl.)** | Glasses / Eyeglasses | `lay lyoo-NET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20lunettes%20%28f.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Plural (f. pl.)` | Elle porte des lunettes de vue pour lire. *(She wears prescription glasses to read.)* | une paire de lunettes | Always plural in French. Porteur de lunettes = glasses wearer. |

| **les cheveux longs** | Long hair | `lay shuh-VUH LOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20longs&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Sa fille a de magnifiques cheveux longs. *(Her daughter has beautiful long hair.)* | long agrees in m. pl.: longs | Silent 'gs' in longs [lɔ̃]. Feminine form is longue [lɔ̃ɡ]. |
| **les cheveux bouclés** | Curly hair | `lay shuh-VUH boo-KLAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20boucl%C3%A9s&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Il a les cheveux bouclés et indisciplinés. *(He has curly, unruly hair.)* | bouclé agrees in m. pl.: bouclés | Derived from boucler (to curl) [bukle]. |
| **les cheveux raides** | Straight hair | `lay shuh-VUH RED` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20cheveux%20raides&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (m. pl.)` | Ses cheveux sont tout à fait raides. *(Her hair is completely straight.)* | raide agrees in m. pl.: raides | Pronounced [ʁɛd]. Spelled 'raides' with -s in plural. |
| **les taches de rousseur (f. pl.)** | Freckles | `lay TAHSH duh roo-SUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20taches%20de%20rousseur%20%28f.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Noun phrase (f. pl.)` | L'enfant a des taches de rousseur sur le nez. *(The child has freckles on his nose.)* | une tache de rousseur | Literally 'spots of redness'. Note: tache (spot) vs tâche (task with circumflex). |
| **la taille** | Height / Size / Waist | `lah TAH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20taille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle est votre taille ? Je mesure un mètre quatre-vingts. *(What is your height? I measure 1m80.)* | les tailles | Liquid -aille [taj]. Dual meaning: clothing size and person's height. |
| **le poids** | Weight | `luh PWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20poids&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Son poids est resté stable cette année. *(His weight has remained stable this year.)* | les poids (invariable in plural) | Silent 'ds' [pwa]. Invariable in plural: un poids, des poids. |
| **mesurer** | To measure / To be (height) tall | `muh-zyoo-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mesurer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Combien mesures-tu ? — Je mesure un mètre soixante-quinze. *(How tall are you? — I am 1.75m tall.)* | je mesure, tu mesures, il mesure, nous mesurons, vous mesurez, ils mesurent | Regular -er verb. In French, use mesurer for height, not être. |
| **peser** | To weigh | `puh-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=peser&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | Le colis pèse trois kilos. *(The parcel weighs three kilos.)* | je pèse, tu pèses, il pèse, nous pesons, vous pesez, ils pèsent | Stem changer: 'e' changes to 'è' before silent endings (je pèse [pɛz], but nous pesons [pəzɔ̃]). |
| **ressembler à** | To look like / To resemble | `ruh-sahn-BLAY ah` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ressembler%20%C3%A0&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Il ressemble beaucoup à son père. *(He looks a lot like his father.)* | je ressemble, tu ressembles, il ressemble, nous ressemblons, vous ressemblez, ils ressemblent | Requires preposition 'à' (ressembler à quelqu'un), unlike English 'resemble someone'. |
| **porter** | To wear / To carry | `por-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=porter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Elle porte un chapeau élégant aujourd'hui. *(She is wearing an elegant hat today.)* | je porte, tu portes, il porte, nous portons, vous portez, ils portent | Regular verb [pɔʁte]. Means both 'to wear' (clothes) and 'to carry'. |
| **élégant / élégante** | Elegant / Stylish | `ay-lay-GAHN / ay-lay-GAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9l%C3%A9gant%20/%20%C3%A9l%C3%A9gante&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cet homme est toujours très élégant dans son costume. *(This man is always very elegant in his suit.)* | élégants / élégantes | Masculine silent 't' [eleɡɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [eleɡɑ̃t]. |
| **musclé / musclée** | Muscular | `myoo-SKLAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=muscl%C3%A9%20/%20muscl%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le gymnaste a un dos très musclé. *(The gymnast has a very muscular back.)* | musclés / musclées | Pronounced identically m./f. [myskle]. Regular agreement. |

## 13. Personality Traits and Emotions (Les traits de caractère et émotions)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **gentil / gentille** | Kind / Nice | `zhahn-TEE / zhahn-TEEY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gentil%20/%20gentille&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle est toujours très gentille avec ses voisins. *(She is always very kind to her neighbors.)* | gentils / gentilles | Masculine silent 'l' [ʒɑ̃ti]; feminine liquid 'lle' [ʒɑ̃tij]. Preposition: gentil avec. |
| **méchant / méchante** | Mean / Nasty / Wicked | `may-SHAHN / may-SHAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=m%C3%A9chant%20/%20m%C3%A9chante&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ne sois pas méchant avec ton petit frère. *(Don't be mean to your little brother.)* | méchants / méchantes | Masculine silent 't' [meʃɑ̃]; feminine sounds 't' [meʃɑ̃t]. |
| **intelligent / intelligente** | Intelligent / Smart | `a(n)-tay-lee-ZHAHN / a(n)-tay-lee-ZHAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=intelligent%20/%20intelligente&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est une élève brillante et intelligente. *(She is a brilliant and intelligent pupil.)* | intelligents / intelligentes | Masculine nasal [ɛ̃teliʒɑ̃]; feminine [ɛ̃teliʒɑ̃t]. |
| **travailleur / travailleuse** | Hardworking | `trah-vah-YUHR / trah-vah-YUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=travailleur%20/%20travailleuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cet artisan est sérieux et très travailleur. *(This craftsman is serious and very hardworking.)* | travailleurs / travailleuses | Pattern -eur -> -euse [tʁavajœʁ] / [tʁavajøz]. |
| **paresseux / paresseuse** | Lazy | `pah-reh-SUH / pah-reh-SUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=paresseux%20/%20paresseuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Le chat reste paresseux sur le canapé. *(The cat stays lazy on the sofa.)* | paresseux (invariable) / paresseuses | Masculine ends in 'x' (invariable in plural); feminine takes -se [paʁɛsøz]. |
| **timide** | Shy / Timid | `tee-MEED` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=timide&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'enfant est un peu timide devant les inconnus. *(The child is a bit shy in front of strangers.)* | timides | Epicene adjective ending in -e [timid]. |
| **bavard / bavarde** | Talkative / Chatty | `bah-VAHR / bah-VAHRD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bavard%20/%20bavarde&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ma sœur est très bavarde au téléphone. *(My sister is very talkative on the phone.)* | bavards / bavardes | Masculine silent 'd' [bavaʁ]; feminine sounds 'd' [bavaʁd]. |
| **calme** | Calm / Quiet / Peaceful | `KAHLM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=calme&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | J'apprécie les soirées calmes à la maison. *(I appreciate quiet evenings at home.)* | calmes | Epicene ending in -e [kalm]. Can describe places or people. |
| **nerveux / nerveuse** | Nervous / Edgy | `nair-VUH / nair-VUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=nerveux%20/%20nerveuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il est nerveux avant son entretien d'embauche. *(He is nervous before his job interview.)* | nerveux (invariable) / nerveuses | Pattern -eux -> -euse [nɛʁvø] / [nɛʁvøz]. |
| **drôle** | Funny / Amusing | `DROHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=dr%C3%B4le&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce comédien raconte des histoires très drôles. *(This comedian tells very funny stories.)* | drôles | Circumflex on 'ô' [dʁol]. Epicene ending in -e. |
| **sérieux / sérieuse** | Serious / Reliable | `say-RYUH / say-RYUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%C3%A9rieux%20/%20s%C3%A9rieuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | C'est un employé sérieux sur qui on peut compter. *(He is a serious employee you can count on.)* | sérieux (invariable) / sérieuses | Masculine plural invariable: des hommes sérieux. |
| **généreux / généreuse** | Generous | `zhay-nay-RUH / zhay-nay-RUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=g%C3%A9n%C3%A9reux%20/%20g%C3%A9n%C3%A9reuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle fait des dons généreux aux associations. *(She makes generous donations to charities.)* | généreux (invariable) / généreuses | Masculine plural invariable: des cœurs généreux. |

[pɔli]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **impoli / impolie** | Impolite / Rude | `a(n)-poh-LEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=impoli%20/%20impolie&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Couper la parole est très impoli. *(Interrupting someone is very rude.)* | impolis / impolies | Nasal [ɛ̃pɔli]. Prefix im- denotes opposite. |

| **impatient / impatiente** | Impatient / Eager | `a(n)-pah-SYAHN / a(n)-pah-SYAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=impatient%20/%20impatiente&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Les enfants sont impatients d'ouvrir leurs cadeaux. *(The children are impatient to open their gifts.)* | impatients / impatientes | Expression: être impatient de + infinitive. |
| **curieux / curieuse** | Curious / Inquisitive | `kyoo-RYUH / kyoo-RYUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=curieux%20/%20curieuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle est très curieuse de découvrir de nouvelles cultures. *(She is very curious to discover new cultures.)* | curieux (invariable) / curieuses | Meaning changes with position: un curieux homme (a strange man) vs un homme curieux (an inquisitive man). |
| **optimiste** | Optimistic | `ohp-tee-MEEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=optimiste&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il garde une attitude optimiste face aux difficultés. *(He keeps an optimistic attitude towards difficulties.)* | optimistes | Epicene ending in -iste [ɔptimist]. |
| **pessimiste** | Pessimistic | `peh-see-MEEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pessimiste&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ne sois pas si pessimiste, tout ira bien. *(Don't be so pessimistic, everything will be fine.)* | pessimistes | Epicene ending in -iste [pɛsimist]. |
| **heureux / heureuse** | Happy / Glad | `uh-RUH / uh-RUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=heureux%20/%20heureuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Nous sommes très heureux de vous accueillir. *(We are very happy to welcome you.)* | heureux (invariable) / heureuses | Silent 'h' (h muet): liaison with [z] (un‿heureux événement [œ̃.n‿øʁø]). |
| **triste** | Sad / Gloomy | `TREEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=triste&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Elle est triste parce que son amie déménage. *(She is sad because her friend is moving.)* | tristes | Epicene ending in -e [tʁist]. |
| **en colère** | Angry / Mad | `ahn koh-LAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=en%20col%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Prepositional Adjective` | Le patron est en colère après cette erreur. *(The boss is angry after this mistake.)* | Invariable (reste 'en colère') | Liaison: en‿colère no liaison. Noun la colère takes accent grave on second 'è'. |
| **fatigué / fatiguée** | Tired / Exhausted | `fah-tee-GAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fatigu%C3%A9%20/%20fatigu%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je suis fatigué après cette longue journée de travail. *(I am tired after this long workday.)* | fatigués / fatiguées | Pronounced identically m./f. [fatiɡe]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **surpris / surprise** | Surprised | `soor-PREE / soor-PREEZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=surpris%20/%20surprise&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Il a été très surpris par cette bonne nouvelle. *(He was very surprised by this good news.)* | surpris (invariable) / surprises | Masculine ends in 's' (invariable in plural); feminine sounds 'z' [syʁpʁiz]. |
| **inquiet / inquiète** | Worried / Anxious | `a(n)-KYAY / a(n)-KYET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=inquiet%20/%20inqui%C3%A8te&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La mère est inquiète quand son fils rentre tard. *(The mother is worried when her son comes home late.)* | inquiets / inquiètes | Spelling change: masculine -iet [ɛ̃kjɛ]; feminine -iète with grave accent [ɛ̃kjɛt]. |
| **fier / fière** | Proud | `FYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fier%20/%20fi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Les parents sont fiers de la réussite de leur fille. *(The parents are proud of their daughter's success.)* | fiers / fières | Final 'r' is sounded in masculine: fier [fjɛʁ]; feminine fière [fjɛʁ]. Preposition: fier de. |
| **la joie** | Joy / Gladness | `lah ZHWAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20joie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ses yeux pétillent de joie et de bonheur. *(His eyes sparkle with joy and happiness.)* | les joies | Feminine noun [ʒwa]. Expression: pleurer de joie. |
| **la peur** | Fear | `lah PUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20peur&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il a une grande peur des araignées. *(He has a great fear of spiders.)* | les peurs | Idiom: avoir peur de = to be afraid of (uses avoir, not être). |
| **ressentir** | To feel / To experience (an emotion) | `ruh-sahn-TEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ressentir&op=translate) | `Verb (2nd/3rd group pattern)` | Je ressens une grande satisfaction après l'effort. *(I feel great satisfaction after the effort.)* | je ressens, tu ressens, il ressent, nous ressentons, vous ressentez, ils ressentent | Conjugated like sentir: drops 't' in singular (je ressens). |

## 15. Types of Housing and Accommodations (Le logement)

A natural cavern is 'une grotte'. |

Also called 'la salle de séjour'. |

Dual meaning: room and culinary style/food. |

In France, usually separate from the toilet! |

| **le balcon** | Balcony | `luh bahl-KOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20balcon&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous cultivons des plantes aromatiques sur notre balcon. *(We grow herbs on our balcony.)* | les balcons | Masculine noun [balkɔ̃]. |
| **aérer** | To air out / To ventilate | `ah-ay-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=a%C3%A9rer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | J'aère ma chambre dix minutes chaque matin. *(I air out my bedroom for ten minutes every morning.)* | j'aère, tu aères, il aère, nous aérons, vous aérez, ils aèrent | Stem changer: 'é' switches to 'è' before silent endings (j'aère [aɛʁ]). |
| **ouvrir** | To open | `oo-VREER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ouvrir&op=translate) | `Irregular Verb (3rd group)` | Peux-tu ouvrir la porte pour nos invités ? *(Can you open the door for our guests?)* | j'ouvre, tu ouvres, il ouvre, nous ouvrons, vous ouvrez, ils ouvrent | Conjugates with -er verb endings despite -ir infinitive (j'ouvre, pas j'ouvris). Past participle: ouvert. |
| **fermer** | To close / To shut | `fair-MAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=fermer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | N'oublie pas de fermer les fenêtres avant de partir. *(Don't forget to close the windows before leaving.)* | je ferme, tu fermes, il ferme, nous fermons, vous fermez, ils ferment | Regular -er verb [fɛʁme]. 'Fermer à clé' = to lock. |
| **le garde-manger** | Pantry / Larder | `luh GARD mahn-ZHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20garde-manger&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous stockons les conserves dans le garde-manger. *(We store canned goods in the pantry.)* | les garde-mangers | Compound noun [ɡaʁdmɑ̃ʒe]. Masculine. |

Liaison: des‿étagères [de-z-etaʒɛʁ]. |

Lampe de chevet = bedside lamp. |

Ends in 's', invariable in plural: un tapis, deux tapis. |

Also called 'la glace' (f.). |

'Horloge' = wall clock; 'montre' = wristwatch; 'réveil' = alarm clock. |

Masculine. |

Expression: passer l'aspirateur = to vacuum. |

## 18. Daily Routines and Habits (La routine quotidienne)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la routine quotidienne** | Daily routine | `lah roo-TEEN koh-tee-DYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20routine%20quotidienne&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Ma routine quotidienne commence à six heures et demie. *(My daily routine begins at half past six.)* | les routines quotidiennes | Adjective quotidien -> quotidienne: doubles 'n' in feminine. |
| **se réveiller** | To wake up | `suh ray-vay-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20r%C3%A9veiller&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Je me réveille à sept heures mais je reste au lit. *(I wake up at seven o'clock but I stay in bed.)* | je me réveille, tu te réveilles, il se réveille, nous nous réveillons, vous vous réveillez, ils se réveillent | Liquid -ill- [ʁeveje]. Distinct from se lever (getting out of bed). |
| **se lever** | To get up / To rise | `suh luh-VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20lever&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | Je me lève immédiatement quand mon réveil sonne. *(I get up immediately when my alarm goes off.)* | je me lève, tu te lèves, il se lève, nous nous levons, vous vous levez, ils se lèvent | Stem changer: 'e' changes to 'è' before silent endings (je me lève [lɛv], but nous nous levons [ləvɔ̃]). |
| **faire son lit** | To make one's bed | `FAIR sohn LEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20son%20lit&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb faire` | Je fais mon lit chaque matin avant de quitter ma chambre. *(I make my bed every morning before leaving my room.)* | je fais mon lit, tu fais ton lit, il fait son lit, nous faisons notre lit | Conjugation of irregular faire: nous faisons is pronounced [fəzɔ̃] despite 'ai'! |
| **prendre une douche** | To take a shower | `PRAHN-druh oon DOOSH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=prendre%20une%20douche&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb prendre` | Je prends une douche chaude pour me réveiller. *(I take a warm shower to wake up.)* | je prends, tu prends, il prend, nous prenons, vous prenez, ils prennent | Also reflexive: se doucher (je me douche). Prendre: doubles 'nn' in ils prennent. |
| **se brosser les dents** | To brush one's teeth | `suh broh-SAY lay DAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20brosser%20les%20dents&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Il faut se brosser les dents après chaque repas. *(One must brush one's teeth after every meal.)* | je me brosse les dents, nous nous brossons les dents | French uses definite article (les dents), NOT possessive pronoun (*mes dents). |
| **se laver** | To wash oneself | `suh lah-VAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20laver&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Je me lave les mains avant de passer à table. *(I wash my hands before sitting down at the table.)* | je me lave, tu te laves, il se lave, nous nous lavons, vous vous lavez, ils se lavent | With body parts: Je me lave le visage (NOT *mon visage). |
| **se raser** | To shave | `suh rah-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20raser&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Mon père se rase tous les matins avec un rasoir électrique. *(My father shaves every morning with an electric razor.)* | je me rase, tu te rases, il se rase, nous nous rasons, vous vous rasez, ils se rasent | Regular -er reflexive verb [ʁaze]. Single 's' pronounced [z]. |
| **se coiffer** | To do one's hair / To comb | `suh kwah-FAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20coiffer&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Elle se coiffe soigneusement devant la glace. *(She styles her hair carefully in front of the mirror.)* | je me coiffe, tu te coiffes, il se coiffe, nous nous coiffons, vous vous coiffez, ils se coiffent | Double 'ff' [kwafe]. Also: se peigner (to comb). |
| **s'habiller** | To get dressed | `sah-bee-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27habiller&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Après la douche, je m'habille chaudement. *(After the shower, I dress warmly.)* | je m'habille, tu t'habilles, il s'habille, nous nous habillons, vous vous habillez, ils s'habillent | Elision: je m'habille, tu t'habilles. Liaison: vous vous‿habillez [vuzabije]. |
| **prendre le petit-déjeuner** | To eat breakfast | `PRAHN-druh luh puh-tee day-zhuh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=prendre%20le%20petit-d%C3%A9jeuner&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb` | Nous prenons le petit-déjeuner ensemble en famille. *(We eat breakfast together as a family.)* | je prends le petit-déjeuner, nous prenons le petit-déjeuner | Also used as verb: petit-déjeuner (je petit-déjeune). |
| **boire un café** | To drink a coffee | `BWAHR œ̃ kah-FAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=boire%20un%20caf%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb boire` | Je bois un café bien noir pour commencer la journée. *(I drink a black coffee to start the day.)* | je bois, tu bois, il boit, nous buvons, vous buvez, ils boivent | Irregular verb boire: stem changes to buv- (nous buvons) and boiv- (ils boivent). |
| **quitter la maison** | To leave the house | `kee-TAY lah may-ZOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=quitter%20la%20maison&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb quitter` | Je quitte la maison vers huit heures moins le quart. *(I leave the house around a quarter to eight.)* | je quitte, tu quittes, il quitte, nous quittons, vous quittez, ils quittent | Quitter requires a direct object (quitter la maison); partir does not (partir de la maison). |
| **aller au travail** | To go to work | `ah-LAY oh trah-VYE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=aller%20au%20travail&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb aller` | Il va au travail en métro tous les jours. *(He goes to work by subway every day.)* | je vais, tu vas, il va, nous allons, vous allez, ils vont | Highly irregular verb aller. Note: au travail (à + le = au). |
| **prendre les transports en commun** | To take public transportation | `PRAHN-druh lay trahn-spohr ahn koh-MUH(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=prendre%20les%20transports%20en%20commun&op=translate) | `Expression` | Je préfère prendre les transports en commun plutôt que la voiture. *(I prefer taking public transit rather than the car.)* | je prends les transports... | En commun = in common / public. Liaison: transports‿en [tʁɑ̃spɔʁ-z-ɑ̃]. |
| **arriver à l'heure** | To arrive on time | `ah-ree-VAY ah LUHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=arriver%20%C3%A0%20l%27heure&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb arriver` | Le train arrive toujours à l'heure à destination. *(The train always arrives on time at its destination.)* | j'arrive, tu arrives, il arrive, nous arrivons, vous arrivez, ils arrivent | À l'heure = on time; en avance = early; en retard = late. |
| **commencer le travail** | To start work | `koh-mahn-SAY luh trah-VYE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=commencer%20le%20travail&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb commencer` | Nous commençons le travail à neuf heures précises. *(We start work at nine o'clock sharp.)* | je commence, nous commençons (cedilla ç), vous commencez | Spelling change: takes cedilla 'ç' before 'o' (nous commençons) to keep the [s] sound. |
| **déjeuner** | To have lunch | `day-zhuh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9jeuner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Je déjeune avec mes collègues à la cantine à midi. *(I have lunch with my colleagues at the canteen at noon.)* | je déjeune, tu déjeunes, il déjeune, nous déjeunons, vous déjeunez, ils déjeunent | Regular -er verb [deʒœne]. As noun: le déjeuner. |
| **faire une pause** | To take a break | `FAIR oon POHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20une%20pause&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb faire` | Faisons une petite pause café de dix minutes. *(Let's take a short ten-minute coffee break.)* | je fais une pause, nous faisons une pause | Pause-café is a deeply ingrained cultural ritual in French offices. |
| **terminer sa journée** | To finish one's workday | `tair-mee-NAY sah zhoor-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=terminer%20sa%20journ%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb terminer` | Elle termine sa journée vers dix-huit heures. *(She finishes her workday around six PM.)* | je termine, tu termines, il termine, nous terminons, vous terminez, ils terminent | Synonym of finir sa journée [tɛʁmine]. |
| **rentrer à la maison** | To go back home / To return home | `rahn-TRAY ah lah may-ZOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=rentrer%20%C3%A0%20la%20maison&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb rentrer` | Je rentre à la maison en bus après le travail. *(I go home by bus after work.)* | je rentre, tu rentres, il rentre, nous rentrons, vous rentrez, ils rentrent | Rentrer implies returning to one's own residence (distinct from retourner/revenir). |
| **faire les courses** | To do the grocery shopping | `FAIR lay KOORSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20les%20courses&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Je m'arrête au supermarché pour faire les courses. *(I stop by the supermarket to do the grocery shopping.)* | je fais les courses, nous faisons les courses | Faire les courses = groceries/errands; faire du shopping = leisure/clothes shopping. |
| **préparer le dîner** | To prepare dinner | `pray-pah-RAY luh dee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=pr%C3%A9parer%20le%20d%C3%AEner&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb préparer` | Mon mari prépare le dîner ce soir. *(My husband is preparing dinner tonight.)* | je prépare le dîner, nous préparons le dîner | Regular verb [pʁepaʁe]. |
| **dîner** | To have dinner | `dee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%AEner&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Nous dînons généralement vers vingt heures. *(We usually have dinner around 8:00 PM.)* | je dîne, tu dînes, il dîne, nous dînons, vous dînez, ils dînent | Circumflex on 'î' [dine]. In France, dinner is eaten later than in North America (around 19:30–20:30). |
| **regarder la télévision** | To watch television | `ruh-gar-DAY lah tay-lay-vee-ZYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=regarder%20la%20t%C3%A9l%C3%A9vision&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb regarder` | Après le repas, ils regardent la télévision ensemble. *(After the meal, they watch television together.)* | je regarde, nous regardons, vous regardez | Regarder is a direct transitive verb in French: regarder la télé (no 'at'). |
| **se détendre** | To relax / To unwind | `suh day-TAHN-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20d%C3%A9tendre&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (3rd group)` | J'écoute de la musique douce pour me détendre. *(I listen to soft music to unwind.)* | je me détends, tu te détends, il se détend, nous nous détendons, vous vous détendez, ils se détendent | Conjugates like attendre / vendre. Silent 's'/'d' in singular: je me détends [detɑ̃]. |
| **se déshabiller** | To get undressed | `suh day-zah-bee-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20d%C3%A9shabiller&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Il se déshabille et met son pyjama. *(He undresses and puts on his pyjamas.)* | je me déshabille, nous nous déshabillons | Prefix dés- before vowel/silent h produces [z] sound [dezabije]. |
| **se coucher** | To go to bed / To lie down | `suh koo-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20coucher&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Je me couche tôt ce soir car je suis fatigué. *(I am going to bed early tonight because I'm tired.)* | je me couche, tu te couches, il se couche, nous nous couchons, vous vous couchez, ils se couchent | Regular -er reflexive [kuʃe]. Distinct from s'endormir (falling asleep). |
| **s'endormir** | To fall asleep | `sahn-dor-MEER` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=s%27endormir&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (3rd group)` | Le bébé s'endort rapidement avec une berceuse. *(The baby falls asleep quickly with a lullaby.)* | je m'endors, tu t'endors, il s'endort, nous nous endormons, vous vous endormez, ils s'endorment | Irregular 3rd group: drops 'm' in singular (je m'endors [ɑ̃dɔʁ]). |
| **faire la grasse matinée** | To sleep in (have a lie-in) | `FAIR lah GRAHSS mah-tee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20la%20grasse%20matin%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Le dimanche, j'adore faire la grasse matinée jusqu'à dix heures. *(On Sundays, I love sleeping in until ten o'clock.)* | je fais la grasse matinée, nous faisons la grasse matinée | Charming French idiom: literally 'to make the fat morning' = to sleep in late! |

| **la tâche ménagère** | Household chore | `lah TAHSH may-nah-ZHAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20t%C3%A2che%20m%C3%A9nag%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous nous partageons équitablement les tâches ménagères. *(We share household chores fairly.)* | les tâches ménagères | Spelling: tâche with circumflex = task/chore; tache without circumflex = stain/spot! |
| **faire le ménage** | To do the housecleaning / housework | `FAIR luh may-NAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20le%20m%C3%A9nage&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb faire` | Je fais le ménage de fond en comble le samedi matin. *(I do the housework thoroughly on Saturday morning.)* | je fais le ménage, nous faisons le ménage | Conjugation of faire: nous faisons [fəzɔ̃]. |
| **nettoyer** | To clean | `neh-twah-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=nettoyer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing -yer)` | Il nettoie le plan de travail de la cuisine. *(He cleans the kitchen countertop.)* | je nettoie, tu nettoies, il nettoie, nous nettoyons, vous nettoyez, ils nettoient | Spelling change: 'y' becomes 'i' before silent endings (je nettoie [nɛtwa], but nous nettoyons [nɛtwajɔ̃]). |
| **balayer** | To sweep | `bah-lay-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=balayer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -yer)` | Elle balaie les feuilles mortes sur la terrasse. *(She sweeps the dead leaves on the terrace.)* | je balaie / balaye, nous balayons, vous balayez, ils balaient / balayent | Verbs in -ayer accept both spellings: je balaie or je balaye. |

Expression: 'un coup de balai' = a quick sweep. |

Aspirateur starts with vowel [aspiʁatœʁ]. |
| **passer la serpillière** | To mop the floor | `pah-SAY lah sair-pee-YAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=passer%20la%20serpilli%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb passer` | Après avoir balayé, elle passe la serpillière sur le carrelage. *(After sweeping, she mops the tile floor.)* | je passe la serpillière, nous passons la serpillière | Liquid -ill- [sɛʁpijɛʁ]. Feminine noun. |
| **la serpillière** | Floor mop / Floor cloth | `lah sair-pee-YAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20serpilli%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Rince bien la serpillière dans le seau d'eau. *(Rinse the floor cloth well in the bucket of water.)* | les serpillières | Spelled serpillière or serpillère. Feminine. |
| **épousseter** | To dust | `ay-poos-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9pousseter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing -eter)` | J'époussette les étagères de la bibliothèque. *(I dust the shelves of the bookcase.)* | j'époussette, tu époussettes, il époussette, nous époussetons, vous époussetez, ils époussettent | Stem changer: doubles 'tt' before silent endings (j'époussette [epusɛt]). Also: faire la poussière. |
| **la poussière** | Dust | `lah poo-SYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20poussi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il y a beaucoup de poussière sous les meubles. *(There is a lot of dust under the furniture.)* | les poussières | Feminine noun [pusjɛʁ]. Expression: faire la poussière = to dust. |
| **laver le sol** | To wash the floor | `lah-VAY luh SOHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=laver%20le%20sol&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb laver` | Je lave le sol de la cuisine avec de l'eau chaude et du savon. *(I wash the kitchen floor with warm water and soap.)* | je lave le sol, nous lavons le sol | Regular -er verb laver [lave]. |
| **laver les vitres** | To clean the windows | `lah-VAY lay VEET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=laver%20les%20vitres&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb laver` | Le soleil brille, c'est le moment de laver les vitres. *(The sun is shining, it's time to clean the windows.)* | je lave les vitres, nous lavons les vitres | Vitre = window pane; fenêtre = window structure. |

Doubles 'ss' and 'll'. |

'Serviette' = table napkin; 'torchon' = kitchen dish towel. |

Linge = washable textiles/clothes. |

Single 's' pronounced [z]. |

Very popular weekend hobby in France. |
| **propre** | Clean (or 'own' before noun) | `PROH-pruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=propre&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La cuisine est impeccablement propre après le ménage. *(The kitchen is impeccably clean after the cleaning.)* | propres | Meaning change by position: 'une chambre propre' = clean room; 'ma propre chambre' = my own room! |
