## 20. Meals and Dining Habits (Les repas)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le repas** | Meal | `luh ruh-PAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20repas&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le repas gastronomique des Français est classé à l'UNESCO. *(The French gastronomic meal is UNESCO-listed.)* | les repas (invariable in plural) | Silent 's' [ʁəpa]. Ends in 's', invariable in plural: un repas, des repas. |
| **le petit-déjeuner** | Breakfast | `luh puh-tee day-zhuh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20petit-d%C3%A9jeuner&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Au petit-déjeuner, je mange un croissant et bois un café. *(At breakfast, I eat a croissant and drink coffee.)* | les petits-déjeuners | Silent 't' in petit [pətideʒœne]. Traditional French breakfast is sweet, not savory. |
| **le déjeuner** | Lunch | `luh day-zhuh-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%A9jeuner&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le déjeuner en France dure souvent au moins une heure. *(Lunch in France often lasts at least one hour.)* | les déjeuners | Masculine noun [deʒœne]. Traditionally the main meal of the day. |
| **le goûter** | Afternoon snack (for children) | `luh goo-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20go%C3%BBter&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | À quatre heures, les enfants prennent leur goûter. *(At 4:00 PM, children have their afternoon snack.)* | les goûters | Circumflex on 'û' [ɡute]. Also called 'le quatre-heures'. Often bread and chocolate. |
| **le dîner** | Dinner / Evening meal | `luh dee-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%AEner&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous partageons un dîner convivial entre amis. *(We share a friendly dinner among friends.)* | les dîners | Circumflex on 'î' [dine]. Typically eaten between 19:30 and 21:00 in France. |
| **l'apéritif / l'apéro (m.)** | Pre-dinner drink & snack / Aperitif | `lah-pay-ree-TEEF / lah-pay-ROH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ap%C3%A9ritif%20/%20l%27ap%C3%A9ro%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Prendre l'apéro est une tradition conviviale incontournable en France. *(Having an apéro is an essential friendly tradition in France.)* | les apéritifs / les apéros | Sounded 'f' [apeʁitif]; colloquially abbreviated everywhere to 'l'apéro'. |
| **l'entrée (f.)** | Starter / First course / Appetizer | `lahn-TRAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27entr%C3%A9e%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Comme entrée, je choisis une soupe à l'oignon. *(As a starter, I choose onion soup.)* | les entrées | False friend for Americans: 'entrée' in French = starter/appetizer, NOT the main course! |
| **le plat principal** | Main course / Main dish | `luh PLAH pra(n)-see-PAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20plat%20principal&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le plat principal est un filet de poisson grillé. *(The main course is a grilled fish fillet.)* | les plats principaux (irregular -aux) | Adjectives ending in -al form plural in -aux: principal -> principaux. |
| **le fromage** | Cheese | `luh froh-MAHZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fromage&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La France produit plus de mille variétés de fromages. *(France produces over a thousand varieties of cheeses.)* | les fromages | Nouns in -age are masculine [fʁɔmaʒ]. Served after main course and BEFORE dessert in France. |
| **le dessert** | Dessert | `luh day-SAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20dessert&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En dessert, nous dégustons une tarte aux pommes maison. *(For dessert, we enjoy a homemade apple tart.)* | les desserts | Silent 't' [desɛʁ]. Doubles 'ss' produces [s] sound (unlike le désert [dezɛʁ] = desert). |
| **le couvert** | Place setting / Cutlery set | `luh koo-VAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20couvert&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mets quatre couverts sur la table pour nos invités. *(Set four places at the table for our guests.)* | les couverts | Sounded 't' [kuvɛʁt] in feminine, but as noun sounded 'r' silent 't' [kuvɛʁ]. |
| **l'assiette (f.)** | Plate / Dish | `lah-SYET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27assiette%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Pose l'assiette creuse sur l'assiette plate. *(Put the soup plate on top of the dinner plate.)* | les assiettes | Feminine noun [asjɛt]. Doubles 'tt'. Assiette creuse = soup bowl/deep plate; assiette plate = flat plate. |
| **la fourchette** | Fork | `lah foor-SHET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20fourchette&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | En France, on place la fourchette à gauche de l'assiette. *(In France, the fork is placed to the left of the plate.)* | les fourchettes | Feminine noun [fuʁʃɛt]. |
| **le couteau** | Knife | `luh koo-TOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20couteau&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le couteau se place à droite de l'assiette, lame vers l'intérieur. *(The knife is placed to the right of the plate, blade facing inwards.)* | Irregular plural: les couteaux (takes -x) | Nouns in -eau take -x in plural [kuto]. |
| **la cuillère / cuiller** | Spoon | `lah kwee-YAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cuill%C3%A8re%20/%20cuiller&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Prends une petite cuillère pour manger ton yaourt. *(Take a teaspoon to eat your yogurt.)* | les cuillères | Both spellings acceptable [kɥijɛʁ]. Petite cuillère = teaspoon; cuillère à soupe = tablespoon. |
| **le verre** | Glass / Drinking glass | `luh VAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20verre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Remplis mon verre d'eau fraîche, s'il te plaît. *(Fill my glass with cold water, please.)* | les verres | Homophones: le verre (glass), le ver (worm), vert (green), vers (towards)! |
| **la tasse** | Cup / Mug | `lah TAHSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tasse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je bois une tasse de thé vert chaque après-midi. *(I drink a cup of green tea every afternoon.)* | les tasses | Feminine noun [tas]. Doubles 'ss'. |
| **la serviette** | Table napkin / Towel | `lah sair-VYET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20serviette&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Mets ta serviette sur tes genoux avant de manger. *(Put your napkin on your lap before eating.)* | les serviettes | Dual meaning: serviette de table = napkin; serviette de bain = bath towel. |
| **la carafe d'eau** | Water pitcher / Carafe of water | `lah kah-RAHF DOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20carafe%20d%27eau&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Une carafe d'eau et une corbeille de pain, s'il vous plaît. *(A pitcher of tap water and a bread basket, please.)* | les carafes d'eau | In French restaurants, 'une carafe d'eau' is free tap water by law! |
| **le sel** | Salt | `luh SEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20sel&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Passe-moi le sel et le poivre, s'il te plaît. *(Pass me the salt and pepper, please.)* | les sels | Sounded 'l' [sɛl]. Masculine noun. |
| **le poivre** | Pepper (spice) | `luh PWAH-vruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20poivre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ajoute une pincée de poivre noir moulu. *(Add a pinch of ground black pepper.)* | les poivres | Spice is le poivre; the vegetable bell pepper is 'le poivron'. |
| **l'huile d'olive (f.)** | Olive oil | `LWHEEL doh-LEEV` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27huile%20d%27olive%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'assaisonne la salade avec de l'huile d'olive et du vinaigre. *(I dress the salad with olive oil and vinegar.)* | les huiles d'olive | Silent 'h' in huile [ɥil dɔliv]. Feminine noun. |
| **le vinaigre** | Vinegar | `luh vee-NEG-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vinaigre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le vinaigre balsamique relève le goût des tomates. *(Balsamic vinegar enhances the taste of tomatoes.)* | les vinaigres | Etymology: vin + aigre = sour wine! Masculine. |
| **mettre la table** | To set the table | `MET-ruh lah TAH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=mettre%20la%20table&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb mettre` | Les enfants aident à mettre la table avant le dîner. *(The children help set the table before dinner.)* | je mets la table, nous mettons la table | Synonym of 'mettre le couvert'. Irregular verb mettre. |
| **débarrasser la table** | To clear the table | `day-bah-rah-SAY lah TAH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9barrasser%20la%20table&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb débarrasser` | Après le repas, tout le monde aide à débarrasser la table. *(After the meal, everyone helps clear the table.)* | je débarrasse, nous débarrassons | Regular -er verb [debaʁase]. Doubles 'rr' and 'ss'. |
| **avoir faim** | To be hungry | `ah-VWAHR FA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20faim&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | J'ai très faim, quand est-ce qu'on mange ? *(I am very hungry, when are we eating?)* | j'ai faim, tu as faim, il a faim, nous avons faim, vous avez faim, ils ont faim | Uses avoir, NOT être (*je suis faim is a grave error). Nasal [fɛ̃]. Silent 'm'. |
| **avoir soif** | To be thirsty | `ah-VWAHR SWAHF` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20soif&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Après la course au soleil, j'ai très soif. *(After running in the sun, I'm very thirsty.)* | j'ai soif, tu as soif, il a soif, nous avons soif, vous avez soif, ils ont soif | Uses avoir, NOT être (*je suis soif is incorrect). Sounded 'f' [swaf]. |
| **avoir bon appétit** | To have a good appetite / Enjoy one's meal | `ah-VWAHR bohn nah-pay-TEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=avoir%20bon%20app%C3%A9tit&op=translate) | `Expression` | Bon appétit à tous et régalez-vous ! *(Enjoy your meal everyone and enjoy the food!)* | Invariable greeting: Bon appétit ! | Mandatory [n] liaison: Bon‿appétit [bɔ̃.n‿apeti]! Silent 't' at the end. |
| **être rassasié / rassasiée** | To be full / satisfied / sated | `EH-truh rah-sah-ZYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%AAtre%20rassasi%C3%A9%20/%20rassasi%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Merci pour ce festin, je suis tout à fait rassasié. *(Thank you for this feast, I am completely full.)* | rassasiés / rassasiées | Polite way to say 'I am full'. Avoid saying 'je suis plein' (which can mean 'I am drunk' or pregnant for animals!). |
| **se régaler** | To feast / To thoroughly enjoy eating | `suh ray-gah-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20r%C3%A9galer&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group)` | Nous nous sommes régalés avec ce repas délicieux ! *(We thoroughly enjoyed this delicious meal!)* | je me régale, tu te régales, il se régale, nous nous régalons, vous vous régalez, ils se régalent | Expression: C'est un régal ! = It's delicious / a real treat! |

Doubles 'rr'. |

Partitive: du pain (some bread). |

Doubles 'tt'. Also means chopsticks or magic wand! |

Note: un croissant ordinaire vs un croissant au beurre. |

In Brittany and Normandy, salted butter (beurre demi-sel) is standard. |

Invariable in plural because it ends in 'z'. |

Feminine plural. Do not confuse with 'la pâte' (dough/pastry). |

Plural: only 'pommes' takes -s, not terre. |

Feminine noun. |

Gousse d'ail = clove of garlic. |

| **l'orange (f.)** | Orange (fruit) | `loh-RAHNZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27orange%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Pressez une orange pour obtenir du jus frais. *(Squeeze an orange to get fresh juice.)* | les oranges | The fruit noun is feminine (une orange). The color adjective is invariable (des vestes orange). |

Dried raisins in French are 'des raisins secs'. |
| **le citron** | Lemon | `luh see-TROHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20citron&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ajoutez un filet de jus de citron sur le poisson. *(Add a drizzle of lemon juice on the fish.)* | les citrons | Masculine noun [sitʁɔ̃]. Citron vert = lime. |

Dual meaning: fruit and fishing. Expression: avoir la pêche = to feel great! |
| **l'œuf (m.) / les œufs (m. pl.)** | Egg / Eggs | `LUF / lay ZUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C5%93uf%20%28m.%29%20/%20les%20%C5%93ufs%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Irregular Noun (m.)` | Il faut casser trois œufs pour la pâte à crêpes. *(You need to break three eggs for the crepe batter.)* | Irregular pronunciation: un œuf [œf] -> les œufs [ø] | Pronunciation change: singular f is sounded [œf]; plural 'fs' are silent [ø] with [z] liaison: les‿œufs [le-z-ø]! |

Contrast with le poison [pwazɔ̃] (poison with single s)! |

Single 's' pronounced [z]. |

## 22. Drinks and Beverages (Les boissons)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la boisson** | Drink / Beverage | `lah bwah-SOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20boisson&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle boisson souhaitez-vous commander ? *(Which drink would you like to order?)* | les boissons | Doubles 'ss' [bwasɔ̃]. Nouns in -on are usually masculine, but la boisson is feminine! |
| **l'eau (f.)** | Water | `LOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27eau%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Buvez au moins un litre et demi d'eau par jour. *(Drink at least a liter and a half of water per day.)* | Irregular plural: les eaux (takes -x) | Gender exception! Noun in -eau is feminine: l'eau, de l'eau. Liaison: les‿eaux [le-z-o]. |
| **l'eau plate** | Still water / Non-carbonated water | `LOH PLAHT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27eau%20plate&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je préfère l'eau plate à température ambiante. *(I prefer still water at room temperature.)* | les eaux plates | Contrasts with l'eau gazeuse (sparkling water). |
| **l'eau gazeuse / pétillante** | Sparkling water / Carbonated water | `LOH gah-ZUHZ / pay-tee-YAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27eau%20gazeuse%20/%20p%C3%A9tillante&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Servez-moi un verre d'eau gazeuse avec une rondelle de citron. *(Serve me a glass of sparkling water with a slice of lemon.)* | les eaux gazeuses / pétillantes | Popular brands in France: Perrier, San Pellegrino, Badoit. |
| **le café** | Coffee / Espresso | `luh kah-FAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20caf%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En France, si vous demandez un café, on vous sert un expresso. *(In France, if you ask for a coffee, you are served an espresso.)* | les cafés | Masculine noun [kafe]. In French bars, 'un café' default is a single short espresso. |
| **le café au lait** | Coffee with hot milk | `luh kah-FAY oh LEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20caf%C3%A9%20au%20lait&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Elle trempe son croissant dans son café au lait. *(She dips her croissant in her café au lait.)* | les cafés au lait | Silent 't' in lait [lɛ]. Typically drunk from a bowl (un bol) at breakfast in France. |
| **le thé** | Tea | `luh TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20th%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Voulez-vous du thé noir ou du thé vert ? *(Would you like black tea or green tea?)* | les thés | Silent 'h' [te]. Accent aigu on 'é'. |
| **la tisane / l'infusion (f.)** | Herbal tea / Infusion | `lah tee-ZAHN / la(n)-fyoo-ZYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20tisane%20/%20l%27infusion%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Une tisane à la camomille aide à s'endormir le soir. *(A chamomile herbal tea helps with falling asleep at night.)* | les tisanes / les infusions | In France, tisane/infusion is strictly caffeine-free herbal tea (verveine, camomille, menthe). |
| **le lait** | Milk | `luh LEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lait&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ajoutez un peu de lait froid dans la sauce. *(Add a little cold milk into the sauce.)* | les laits | Silent 't' [lɛ]. Lait entier = whole milk; lait demi-écrémé = semi-skimmed. |
| **le chocolat chaud** | Hot chocolate | `luh shoh-koh-LAH SHOH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chocolat%20chaud&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un bon chocolat chaud crémeux réchauffe après le ski. *(A good creamy hot chocolate warms you up after skiing.)* | les chocolats chauds | Silent 't' and 'd' [ʃɔkɔla ʃo]. |
| **le jus** | Juice | `luh ZHYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jus&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Voulez-vous un verre de jus de fruits frais ? *(Would you like a glass of fresh fruit juice?)* | les jus (invariable in plural) | Silent 's' [ʒy]. Invariable in plural because it ends in 's': un jus, des jus. |
| **le jus d'orange** | Orange juice | `luh zhyoo doh-RAHNZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jus%20d%27orange&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Un jus d'orange pressé, s'il vous plaît. *(A freshly squeezed orange juice, please.)* | les jus d'orange | 'Pressé' specifies freshly squeezed rather than bottled juice. |
| **le jus de pomme** | Apple juice | `luh zhyoo duh POHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20jus%20de%20pomme&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce jus de pomme artisanal de Normandie est délicieux. *(This artisanal apple juice from Normandy is delicious.)* | les jus de pomme | Masculine noun phrase. |
| **la limonade** | Lemonade (carbonated, clear, sweet) | `lah lee-moh-NAHD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20limonade&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La limonade française est gazeuse et sucrée. *(French lemonade is fizzy and sweet.)* | les limonades | False friend: in France, limonade is sparkling lemon soda (like Sprite/7Up), NOT still homemade lemon juice (citronnade). |
| **le soda** | Soda / Fizzy soft drink | `luh soh-DAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20soda&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il est conseillé de limiter la consommation de sodas sucrés. *(It is advisable to limit consumption of sugary sodas.)* | les sodas | Masculine noun [sɔda]. |
| **le sirop** | Flavored syrup / Cordial | `luh see-ROH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20sirop&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les enfants adorent l'eau avec du sirop de grenadine. *(Children love water with grenadine syrup.)* | les sirops | Silent 'p' [siʁo]. Common French café order: un sirop à l'eau (syrup with tap water). |
| **la bière** | Beer | `lah BYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Garçon, une bière blonde pression, s'il vous plaît ! *(Waiter, a draught lager, please!)* | les bières | Feminine noun [bjɛʁ]. Bière pression = draught/tap beer; bière en bouteille = bottled. |
| **le vin** | Wine | `luh VA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce domaine produit un vin d'appellation contrôlée. *(This estate produces an appellation-controlled wine.)* | les vins | Nasal vowel [vɛ̃]. Core element of French gastronomic culture. |
| **le vin rouge** | Red wine | `luh va(n) ROOZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vin%20rouge&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le vin rouge accompagne parfaitement la viande et le fromage. *(Red wine goes perfectly with meat and cheese.)* | les vins rouges | Served at room temperature (chambré). |
| **le vin blanc** | White wine | `luh va(n) BLAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vin%20blanc&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Ce vin blanc sec se marie à merveille avec les fruits de mer. *(This dry white wine pairs wonderfully with seafood.)* | les vins blancs | Silent 'c' in blanc [blɑ̃]. Served chilled. |
| **le vin rosé** | Rosé wine | `luh va(n) roh-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20vin%20ros%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En été, on déguste un verre de rosé frais en terrasse. *(In summer, we enjoy a chilled glass of rosé on the terrace.)* | les vins rosés | Especially famous from Provence. |
| **le champagne** | Champagne | `luh shahn-PAH-nyuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20champagne&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous débouchons une bouteille de champagne pour fêter l'événement. *(We uncork a bottle of champagne to celebrate the event.)* | les champagnes | Masculine for the beverage (le champagne); feminine for the historical region (la Champagne). |
| **le cocktail** | Cocktail | `luh kohk-TEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cocktail&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le barman prépare un cocktail rafraîchissant sans alcool. *(The bartender prepares a refreshing non-alcoholic cocktail.)* | les cocktails | Borrowed word, masculine in French [kɔktɛl]. |
| **les glaçons (m. pl.)** | Ice cubes | `lay glah-SOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20gla%C3%A7ons%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Avec des glaçons, s'il vous plaît. *(With ice cubes, please.)* | un glaçon -> des glaçons | Cedilla ç ensures [s] sound before 'o' [ɡlasɔ̃]. |
| **la paille** | Drinking straw | `lah PYE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20paille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Puis-je avoir une paille pour boire mon soda ? *(May I have a straw to drink my soda?)* | les pailles | Liquid -aille [paj]. Dual meaning: drinking straw and agricultural straw/hay. |
| **la bouteille** | Bottle | `lah boo-TEH-yuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bouteille&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous commandons une bouteille d'eau minérale pour la table. *(We order a bottle of mineral water for the table.)* | les bouteilles | Liquid -eille [butɛj]. Feminine noun. |
| **la canette** | Can / Tin (of drink) | `lah kah-NET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20canette&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Prends une canette de thé glacé dans le distributeur. *(Get a can of iced tea from the vending machine.)* | les canettes | Feminine noun [kanɛt]. Doubles 'tt'. |
| **boire** | To drink | `BWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=boire&op=translate) | `Irregular Verb (3rd group)` | Il est important de boire régulièrement tout au long de la journée. *(It is important to drink regularly throughout the day.)* | je bois, tu bois, il boit, nous buvons, vous buvez, ils boivent | Past participle: bu. Stem changes: bois / buv- / boiv-. |
| **trinquer** | To clink glasses / To toast / To cheers | `tra(n)-KAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=trinquer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Trinquons à votre santé et à notre amitié ! *(Let's toast to your health and our friendship!)* | je trinque, tu trinques, il trinque, nous trinquons, vous trinquez, ils trinquent | Custom: when clinking glasses in France, look the person straight in the eye! |
| **étancher sa soif** | To quench one's thirst | `ay-tahn-SHAY sah SWAHF` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9tancher%20sa%20soif&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Cette eau bien fraîche étanche parfaitement ma soif. *(This very cold water quenches my thirst perfectly.)* | j'étanche ma soif, nous étanchons notre soif | Regular verb étancher [etɑ̃ʃe]. Sounded 'f' in soif [swaf]. |

## 23. Ordering at a Café or Restaurant (Au café et au restaurant)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le restaurant** | Restaurant | `luh res-toh-RAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20restaurant&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous dînons dans un restaurant typique du quartier latin. *(We are dining in a typical restaurant in the Latin Quarter.)* | les restaurants | Masculine noun [ʁɛstɔʁɑ̃]. Colloquially called 'le resto'. Silent 't'. |
| **le café / le bistrot** | Café / Bistro | `luh kah-FAY / luh bees-TROH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20caf%C3%A9%20/%20le%20bistrot&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Retrouvons-nous au bistrot du coin à quatorze heures. *(Let's meet at the corner bistro at 2:00 PM.)* | les cafés / les bistrots | Silent 't' in bistrot [bistʁo]. Center of French social life. |
| **la terrasse** | Outdoor café terrace / Sidewalk seating | `lah tay-RAHSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20terrasse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Une table en terrasse pour deux personnes, s'il vous plaît. *(A table on the terrace for two people, please.)* | les terrasses | In French café culture, sitting en terrasse is slightly more expensive than at the bar (au comptoir). |
| **la carte / le menu** | The menu / Set menu | `lah KART / luh muh-NYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20carte%20/%20le%20menu&op=translate) | `Feminine / Masculine (f./m.)` | Puis-je voir la carte des vins et des desserts ? *(May I see the wine and dessert list?)* | les cartes / les menus | Crucial distinction: 'la carte' = the full menu (à la carte); 'le menu' = a fixed-price multi-course combo meal! |
| **la formule / le menu du jour** | Set meal / Daily special combo | `lah for-MYOOL / luh muh-nyoo dyoo ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20formule%20/%20le%20menu%20du%20jour&op=translate) | `Feminine / Masculine (f./m.)` | Je vais prendre la formule entrée-plat à quinze euros. *(I'll have the starter + main course combo at 15 euros.)* | les formules | Usually offered at lunch on weekdays: entrée + plat or plat + dessert. |
| **le plat du jour** | Today's special / Daily special | `luh PLAH dyoo ZHOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20plat%20du%20jour&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Quel est le plat du jour aujourd'hui ? — C'est du saumon grillé. *(What is today's special? — It's grilled salmon.)* | les plats du jour | Freshly prepared dish changed daily by the chef. |
| **la réservation** | Reservation / Booking | `lah ray-zair-vah-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20r%C3%A9servation&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'ai une réservation au nom de Dupont pour quatre personnes. *(I have a reservation under the name Dupont for four people.)* | les réservations | Nouns in -tion are feminine [ʁezeʁvasjɔ̃]. |
| **réserver une table** | To book / reserve a table | `ray-zair-VAY oon TAH-bluh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=r%C3%A9server%20une%20table&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb réserver` | Je voudrais réserver une table pour ce soir à vingt heures. *(I would like to book a table for tonight at 8:00 PM.)* | je réserve, nous réservons, vous réservez | Stem changer: 'é' switches to 'è' before silent endings (je réserve [ʁezɛʁv]). |
| **commander** | To order (food/drink) | `koh-mahn-DAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=commander&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Êtes-vous prêts à commander ou désirez-vous quelques minutes ? *(Are you ready to order or would you like a few minutes?)* | je commande, tu commandes, il commande, nous commandons, vous commandez, ils commandent | Regular -er verb [kɔmɑ̃de]. Commandeur = customer making the order. |
| **Je voudrais...** | I would like... (polite request) | `zhuh voo-DREH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=Je%20voudrais...&op=translate) | `Expression / Conditional` | Je voudrais une carafe d'eau et l'addition, s'il vous plaît. *(I would like a jug of water and the bill, please.)* | Conditional of vouloir: je voudrais, nous voudrions | Always say 'Je voudrais' instead of blunt 'Je veux' (I want) when ordering! |
| **Pour moi, ce sera...** | For me, I will have... | `poor MWAH suh suh-RAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=Pour%20moi%2C%20ce%20sera...&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Ordering Phrase` | Pour moi, ce sera le plat du jour avec des frites. *(For me, I'll have the dish of the day with fries.)* | Ce sera = future of être (invariable idiom) | Extremely natural French phrasing used by native speakers when placing orders. |
| **l'addition (f.)** | The bill / The check | `lah-dee-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27addition%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | L'addition, s'il vous plaît, monsieur ! *(The bill, please, sir!)* | les additions | Feminine noun [adisjɔ̃]. Do not ask for 'la facture' in a restaurant; restaurants use 'l'addition'. |
| **demander l'addition** | To ask for the bill | `duh-mahn-DAY lah-dee-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=demander%20l%27addition&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb demander` | Nous avons fini, nous pouvons demander l'addition. *(We are finished, we can ask for the bill.)* | je demande l'addition, nous demandons l'addition | Demander is regular -er verb [dəmɑ̃de]. |
| **le pourboire** | Tip / Gratuity | `luh poor-BWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pourboire&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le service est compris, mais nous laissons un pourboire de deux euros. *(Service is included, but we leave a 2 euro tip.)* | les pourboires | Etymology: pour + boire = money given to have a drink! Masculine. |
| **laisser un pourboire** | To leave a tip | `leh-SAY œ̃ poor-BWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=laisser%20un%20pourboire&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb laisser` | Le serveur était si aimable que j'ai laissé un bon pourboire. *(The waiter was so pleasant that I left a good tip.)* | je laisse, nous laissons, vous laissez | Regular -er verb laisser [lɛse]. Liaison: un‿pourboire no liaison. |
| **payer par carte bancaire** | To pay by debit/credit card | `pay-YAY par KART bahn-KAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=payer%20par%20carte%20bancaire&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb payer` | Puis-je payer par carte bancaire ou seulement en espèces ? *(Can I pay by card or only cash?)* | je paie / paye, nous payons, vous payez | Abbreviated as 'par carte' or 'par CB'. Payer accepts both 'je paie' and 'je paye'. |
| **payer en espèces / en liquide** | To pay in cash | `pay-YAY ahn nes-PESS / ahn lee-KEED` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=payer%20en%20esp%C3%A8ces%20/%20en%20liquide&op=translate) | `Expression` | Je n'ai pas de monnaie, je paie en liquide. *(I don't have change, I pay in cash.)* | En espèces / en liquide = invariable idioms | Liaison: en‿espèces [ɑ̃.n‿ɛspɛs] with [n]. 'En liquide' is more colloquial. |
| **le service compris** | Service included (15% by French law) | `luh sair-VEES kohn-PREE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20service%20compris&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | En France, le service est toujours compris dans le prix affiché. *(In France, service is always included in the displayed price.)* | Invariable expression on bills | By French law, a 15% service charge is already included in all restaurant prices! |
| **végétarien / végétarienne** | Vegetarian (m./f.) | `vay-zhay-tah-RYA(N) / vay-zhay-tah-RYEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=v%C3%A9g%C3%A9tarien%20/%20v%C3%A9g%C3%A9tarienne&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Avez-vous des plats végétariens à la carte ? *(Do you have vegetarian dishes on the menu?)* | végétariens / végétariennes | Pattern -ien -> -ienne: doubles 'n' [veʒetaʁjɛ̃] / [veʒetaʁjɛn]. |
| **sans gluten** | Gluten-free | `SAHN glyoo-TEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sans%20gluten&op=translate) | `Adjective phrase (invariable)` | Ce dessert est garanti sans gluten. *(This dessert is guaranteed gluten-free.)* | Invariable phrase | Pronounced [sɑ̃ ɡlytɛn]. Sounded 'en' as [ɛn]. |
| **allergique à** | Allergic to | `ah-lair-ZHEEK ah` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=allergique%20%C3%A0&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je suis très allergique aux arachides et aux fruits de mer. *(I am very allergic to peanuts and shellfish.)* | allergiques à | Epicene ending in -e [alɛʁʒik]. Requires preposition 'à' (allergique à/aux). |
| **la cuisson** | Cooking / Doneness of meat | `lah kwee-SOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20cuisson&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Quelle cuisson désirez-vous pour votre steak ? *(How would you like your steak cooked?)* | les cuissons | Feminine noun [kɥisɔ̃]. The waiter will ask this whenever you order beef. |
| **saignant / saignante** | Rare (meat cooked rare/bloody) | `seh-NYAHN / seh-NYAHNT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=saignant%20/%20saignante&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Je préfère mon faux-filet saignant. *(I prefer my sirloin rare.)* | saignants / saignantes | Derived from saigner (to bleed). Palatal gn [ɲ] [sɛɲɑ̃]. French 'saignant' is quite red/rare! |
| **à point** | Medium (meat cooked medium) | `ah PWA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A0%20point&op=translate) | `Adverbial phrase` | Pour la viande, je la veux à point, s'il vous plaît. *(For the meat, I want it cooked medium, please.)* | Invariable phrase | Silent 't' [a pwɛ̃]. In France, 'à point' is pink and juicy in the center. |
| **bien cuit / bien cuite** | Well-done (meat cooked through) | `BYA(N) KWEE / BYA(N) KWEET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bien%20cuit%20/%20bien%20cuite&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Mon père commande toujours sa viande bien cuite. *(My father always orders his meat well-done.)* | bien cuits / bien cuites | Masculine silent 't' [bjɛ̃ kɥi]; feminine sounds 't' [bjɛ̃ kɥit]. |
| **chaud / chaude** | Hot / Warm (temperature) | `SHOH / SHOHD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=chaud%20/%20chaude&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Attention, l'assiette est très chaude ! *(Careful, the plate is very hot!)* | chauds / chaudes | Masculine silent 'd' [ʃo]; feminine sounds 'd' [ʃod]. 'Chaud' = heat; spicy is 'épicé'. |
| **froid / froide** | Cold (temperature) | `FRWAH / FRWAHD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=froid%20/%20froide&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Cette soupe froide de concombre est parfaite en été. *(This cold cucumber soup is perfect in summer.)* | froids / froides | Masculine silent 'd' [fʁwa]; feminine sounds 'd' [fʁwad]. |
| **délicieux / délicieuse** | Delicious / Delectable | `day-lee-SYUH / day-lee-SYUHZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9licieux%20/%20d%C3%A9licieuse&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce plat de poisson aux herbes est absolument délicieux ! *(This herbal fish dish is absolutely delicious!)* | délicieux (invariable) / délicieuses | Pattern -eux -> -euse [delisjø] / [delisjøz]. Masculine plural invariable. |
| **C'était délicieux !** | It was delicious! | `seh-TAY day-lee-SYUH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=C%27%C3%A9tait%20d%C3%A9licieux%20%21&op=translate) | `Compliment Phrase` | Mes compliments au chef, c'était absolument délicieux ! *(My compliments to the chef, it was absolutely delicious!)* | Invariable phrase | Imparfait tense of c'est [sɛte]. Perfect praise when the waiter clears your plate. |
| **santé !** | Cheers! / To your health! | `sahn-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=sant%C3%A9%20%21&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Levons nos verres à cette belle soirée : santé ! *(Let's raise our glasses to this lovely evening: cheers!)* | Also: À votre santé ! | Standard toast in France [sɑ̃te]. Or informally: 'Tchin-tchin !'. |

## 24. Shops and Commercial Places (Les commerces et magasins)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le magasin** | Shop / Store | `luh mah-gah-ZA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20magasin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les magasins ferment généralement à dix-neuf heures. *(Shops usually close at 7:00 PM.)* | les magasins | Masculine noun [maɡazɛ̃]. False friend: 'magazine' (reading material) is 'une revue' or 'un magazine'. |
| **le commerce** | Shop / Business / Trade / Commerce | `luh koh-MAIRSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20commerce&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous soutenons les petits commerces de notre quartier. *(We support the small shops in our neighborhood.)* | les commerces | Masculine noun [kɔmɛʁs]. Doubles 'mm'. Commerce de proximité = local corner shop. |
| **le supermarché** | Supermarket | `luh soo-pair-mar-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20supermarch%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je fais mes grandes courses au supermarché une fois par semaine. *(I do my big grocery run at the supermarket once a week.)* | les supermarchés | Masculine noun [sypɛʁmaʁʃe]. |
| **l'hypermarché (m.)** | Hypermarket / Giant superstore | `lee-pair-mar-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27hypermarch%C3%A9%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'hypermarché en périphérie vend des produits alimentaires et électroniques. *(The hypermarket on the outskirts sells groceries and electronics.)* | les hypermarchés | Silent 'h' [ipɛʁmaʁʃe]. Hypermarkets (Carrefour, Auchan, Leclerc) are very popular in France. |
| **l'épicerie (f.)** | Grocery store / Corner convenience store | `lay-pees-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9picerie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | L'épicerie du coin est ouverte même le dimanche soir. *(The corner grocery is open even on Sunday evening.)* | les épiceries | Feminine noun [episʁi]. Also called 'l'épicier' (going chez l'épicier). |
| **la boulangerie** | Bakery (bread & viennoiseries) | `lah boo-lahn-zhuh-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20boulangerie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Il achète deux baguettes fraîches à la boulangerie du village. *(He buys two fresh baguettes at the village bakery.)* | les boulangeries | Feminine noun [bulɑ̃ʒʁi]. By law in France, a 'boulangerie' must bake bread on site from scratch! |
| **la pâtisserie** | Pastry shop / Cake shop | `lah pah-tees-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20p%C3%A2tisserie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous avons commandé un éclair au chocolat dans cette pâtisserie. *(We ordered a chocolate eclair in this pastry shop.)* | les pâtisseries | Circumflex on 'â' [patisʁi]. Often combined as 'boulangerie-pâtisserie'. |
| **la boucherie** | Butcher shop | `lah boosh-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20boucherie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le boucher découpe la viande devant le client à la boucherie. *(The butcher cuts the meat in front of the customer at the butcher shop.)* | les boucheries | Feminine noun [buʃʁi]. Preposition: à la boucherie or chez le boucher. |
| **la charcuterie** | Pork butcher's / Delicatessen | `lah shar-koot-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20charcuterie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La charcuterie propose du jambon de pays et des terrines maison. *(The charcuterie offers country ham and homemade terrines.)* | les charcuteries | Feminine noun [ʃaʁkytʁi]. Sells cured meats, sausages, and pates. |

Doubles 'ss' and 'nn'. |
| **la pharmacie** | Pharmacy / Chemist's | `lah far-mah-SEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20pharmacie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La pharmacie de garde est signalée par une croix verte allumée. *(The on-duty pharmacy is indicated by an illuminated green cross.)* | les pharmacies | In France, illuminated flashing green cross marks pharmacies. |
| **la librairie** | Bookstore / Bookshop | `lah lee-bray-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20librairie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'adore flâner entre les rayons de cette librairie indépendante. *(I love browsing through the aisles of this independent bookstore.)* | les librairies | Major false friend: 'librairie' = bookstore (sells books); 'bibliothèque' = library (loans books)! |
| **la papeterie** | Stationery shop | `lah pah-pet-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20papeterie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'achète mes cahiers et stylos à la papeterie. *(I buy my notebooks and pens at the stationery shop.)* | les papeteries | Feminine noun [paptʁi]. Often combined: 'librairie-papeterie'. |

Silent 'd' in marchand [maʁʃɑ̃]. |

| **la boutique de chaussures** | Shoe shop / Footwear boutique | `lah boo-TEEK duh shoh-SYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20boutique%20de%20chaussures&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Elle essaie des sandales d'été dans une boutique de chaussures. *(She is trying on summer sandals in a shoe boutique.)* | les boutiques de chaussures | 'Boutique' is feminine [butik]. Often smaller than 'un magasin'. |
| **la bijouterie** | Jewelry store | `lah bee-zhoot-REE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20bijouterie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La bague en or scintille dans la vitrine de la bijouterie. *(The gold ring sparkles in the jewelry shop window.)* | les bijouteries | Feminine noun [biʒutʁi]. |
| **le fleuriste / chez le fleuriste** | Florist / Flower shop | `luh fluh-REEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fleuriste%20/%20chez%20le%20fleuriste&op=translate) | `Noun (m./f.)` | Il achète un beau bouquet de tulipes chez le fleuriste. *(He buys a pretty bouquet of tulips at the florist's.)* | les fleuristes | French idiom: use 'chez' with shopkeepers (chez le boucher, chez le boulanger, chez le fleuriste). |
| **le centre commercial** | Shopping center / Shopping mall | `luh SAHN-truh koh-mair-SYAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20centre%20commercial&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le centre commercial regroupe plus de cinquante boutiques. *(The shopping center brings together over 50 shops.)* | les centres commerciaux (irregular -aux) | Adjective -al becomes -aux in plural: commercial -> commerciaux. |
| **le marché** | Open-air market / Farmers market | `luh mar-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20march%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous allons au marché le samedi matin pour acheter des fruits frais. *(We go to the market on Saturday morning to buy fresh fruit.)* | les marchés | Masculine noun [maʁʃe]. Core weekly routine in French towns. |
| **le grand magasin** | Department store | `luh GRAHN mah-gah-ZA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20grand%20magasin&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les Galeries Lafayette sont un grand magasin historique de Paris. *(Galeries Lafayette is a historic department store in Paris.)* | les grands magasins | Liaison: grands‿magasins silent 'd'/'s' [ɡʁɑ̃ maɡazɛ̃]. |
| **la vitrine** | Shop window / Display window | `lah vee-TREEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20vitrine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les mannequins sont joliment habillés dans la vitrine. *(The mannequins are nicely dressed in the shop window.)* | les vitrines | Feminine noun [vitʁin]. |
| **faire du lèche-vitrines** | To go window-shopping | `FAIR dyoo LESH vee-TREEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20du%20l%C3%A8che-vitrines&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Expression` | Le samedi après-midi, nous aimons faire du lèche-vitrines au centre-ville. *(On Saturday afternoon, we like window-shopping downtown.)* | je fais du lèche-vitrines, nous faisons du lèche-vitrines | Literally 'to lick the display windows'! Very vivid and authentic French idiom. |

Past participle of ouvrir. |
| **fermé / fermée** | Closed | `fair-MAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=ferm%C3%A9%20/%20ferm%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | La boutique est fermée pour les congés annuels en août. *(The boutique is closed for annual holidays in August.)* | fermés / fermées | Pronounced identically m./f. [fɛʁme]. Feminine adds silent '-e'. |
| **les horaires d'ouverture (m. pl.)** | Opening hours / Business hours | `lay zoh-RAIR doo-vair-TYOOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20horaires%20d%27ouverture%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | Quels sont les horaires d'ouverture de la banque ? *(What are the bank's opening hours?)* | un horaire -> les horaires | Silent 'h' in horaire: mandatory [z] liaison les‿horaires [le-z-ɔʁɛʁ]. |
| **la caisse** | Checkout / Cash register / Cash desk | `lah KESS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20caisse&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Veuillez vous présenter à la caisse numéro trois. *(Please proceed to checkout number three.)* | les caisses | Feminine noun [kɛs]. Doubles 'ss'. 'Passer en caisse' = to go through checkout. |
| **le chariot / le caddie** | Shopping cart / Trolley | `luh shah-RYOH / luh kah-DEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20chariot%20/%20le%20caddie&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il faut insérer une pièce d'un euro pour débloquer le chariot. *(You need to insert a 1 euro coin to unlock the cart.)* | les chariots / les caddies | Silent 't' in chariot [ʃaʁjo]. 'Caddie' is a registered brand name often used generically. |

Online shopping: 'votre panier' = your cart. |
| **faire des achats** | To make purchases / To go shopping | `FAIR day zah-SHAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=faire%20des%20achats&op=translate) | `Expression / Verb faire` | Elle est allée au centre commercial pour faire des achats. *(She went to the shopping mall to make purchases.)* | je fais des achats, nous faisons des achats | Mandatory [z] liaison: des‿achats [de-z-aʃa]. Silent 't' and 's' in achats. |

## 25. Shopping and Asking for Prices (Faire les courses et les prix)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **Combien coûte... ?** | How much does ... cost? | `kohn-BYA(N) KOOT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=Combien%20co%C3%BBte...%20%3F&op=translate) | `Interrogative Phrase` | Pardon madame, combien coûte ce foulard en soie ? *(Excuse me ma'am, how much does this silk scarf cost?)* | Plural: Combien coûtent... ? [kut] | Silent 'e' in coûte [kut] and silent 'ent' in plural coûtent [kut]. |
| **Combien ça coûte ?** | How much is it? / What does it cost? | `kohn-BYA(N) sah KOOT` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=Combien%20%C3%A7a%20co%C3%BBte%20%3F&op=translate) | `Interrogative Phrase` | Cette veste me plaît, combien ça coûte ? *(I like this jacket, how much is it?)* | Informal synonym: C'est combien ? | Everyday question for asking prices anywhere in France. |
| **le prix** | Price | `luh PREE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20prix&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le prix de cet article est très raisonnable. *(The price of this item is very reasonable.)* | les prix (invariable in plural) | Silent 'x' [pʁi]. Ends in 'x', so invariable in plural: un prix, des prix. |
| **cher / chère** | Expensive / Dear | `SHAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=cher%20/%20ch%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | Ce restaurant trois étoiles est trop cher pour notre budget. *(This 3-star restaurant is too expensive for our budget.)* | chers / chères | Sounded 'r' in both genders [ʃɛʁ]. Feminine takes accent grave: chère. Dual meaning: expensive and dear (mon cher ami). |
| **bon marché** | Inexpensive / Cheap / Good value | `bohn mar-SHAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=bon%20march%C3%A9&op=translate) | `Adjective phrase (invariable)` | Ces fruits sont très frais et bon marché. *(These fruits are very fresh and inexpensive.)* | Invariable (never adds -s or -e) | Grammar rule: bon marché is strictly INVARIABLE: des robes bon marché (no -s)! |
| **gratuit / gratuite** | Free of charge | `grah-TWEE / grah-TWEET` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=gratuit%20/%20gratuite&op=translate) | `Adjective (m./f.)` | L'entrée du musée est gratuite le premier dimanche du mois. *(Museum admission is free on the first Sunday of the month.)* | gratuits / gratuites | Masculine silent 't' [ɡʁatɥi]; feminine sounds 't' [ɡʁatɥit]. Note: libre = free (unconstrained); gratuit = free (cost 0€). |
| **la réduction / la remise** | Discount / Reduction / Rebate | `lah ray-dyook-SYOHN / lah ruh-MEEZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20r%C3%A9duction%20/%20la%20remise&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les étudiants bénéficient d'une réduction de vingt pour cent. *(Students benefit from a 20% discount.)* | les réductions / les remises | Nouns in -tion are feminine [ʁedyksjɔ̃]. |
| **les soldes (m. pl.)** | Sales (seasonal clearance sales) | `lay SOHLD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=les%20soldes%20%28m.%20pl.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine Plural (m. pl.)` | J'attends les soldes d'été pour renouveler ma garde-robe. *(I'm waiting for the summer sales to renew my wardrobe.)* | En soldes / faire les soldes | Gender trap! 'Le solde' (clearance sales) is MASCULINE plural: les soldes d'hiver sont très attendus. |
| **en promotion** | On special offer / On sale | `ahn proh-moh-SYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=en%20promotion&op=translate) | `Prepositional Phrase` | Ce paquet de café est en promotion cette semaine. *(This packet of coffee is on special offer this week.)* | Invariable phrase | Liaison: en‿promotion no liaison. Often abbreviated to 'en promo'. |
| **la monnaie** | Small change / Currency / Coins | `lah moh-NEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20monnaie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Avez-vous de la monnaie sur un billet de vingt euros ? *(Do you have change for a 20 euro note?)* | les monnaies | Feminine noun [mɔnɛ]. 'Faire la monnaie' = to make change. |
| **le billet de banque** | Banknote / Paper bill | `luh bee-YAY duh BAHN-kuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20billet%20de%20banque&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il me reste un billet de cinquante euros dans mon portefeuille. *(I have a 50 euro note left in my wallet.)* | les billets de banque | Silent 't' in billet [bije]. Euro banknotes exist in 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 200, and 500€. |
| **la pièce de monnaie** | Coin | `lah PYES duh moh-NEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20pi%C3%A8ce%20de%20monnaie&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Insérez une pièce de deux euros dans le parcmètre. *(Insert a 2 euro coin into the parking meter.)* | les pièces de monnaie | Usually shortened simply to 'une pièce' [pjɛs]. |
| **la carte bancaire** | Debit/Credit card / Bank card | `lah KART bahn-KAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20carte%20bancaire&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le paiement par carte bancaire est accepté à partir de un euro. *(Card payment is accepted from 1 euro upwards.)* | les cartes bancaires | Abbreviated everywhere in France as 'la carte' or 'la CB' [se-be]. |
| **le ticket de caisse / le reçu** | Sales receipt / Cash register receipt | `luh tee-KEH duh KESS / luh ruh-SYOO` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20ticket%20de%20caisse%20/%20le%20re%C3%A7u&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Gardez bien votre ticket de caisse pour tout échange ou remboursement. *(Keep your receipt safe for any exchange or refund.)* | les tickets de caisse / les reçus | Silent 't' in ticket [tikɛ]. Cedilla ç in reçu [ʁəsy]. By recent law, paper receipts are given upon request in France. |
| **payer** | To pay / To pay for | `pay-YAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=payer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -yer)` | Je paie mes achats par carte sans contact. *(I pay for my purchases with contactless card.)* | je paie / paye, tu paies / payes, il paie / paye, nous payons, vous payez, ils paient / payent | Both 'y' and 'i' spellings accepted. Payer is transitive in French: payer quelque chose (no 'for'!). |
| **coûter** | To cost | `koo-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=co%C3%BBter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Combien coûte ce dictionnaire de français ? *(How much does this French dictionary cost?)* | il coûte, ils coûtent | Circumflex on 'û' [kute]. Most frequently used in 3rd person: ça coûte, ils coûtent. |
| **acheter** | To buy / To purchase | `ahsh-TAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=acheter&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | J'achète des fruits et légumes bio chaque semaine. *(I buy organic fruits and vegetables every week.)* | j'achète, tu achètes, il achète, nous achetons, vous achetez, ils achètent | Stem changer: 'e' changes to 'è' before silent endings (j'achète [aʃɛt], but nous achetons [aʃtɔ̃]). |
| **vendre** | To sell | `VAHN-druh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=vendre&op=translate) | `Verb (3rd group regular -re)` | Le commerçant vend des produits du terroir. *(The shopkeeper sells local regional products.)* | je vends, tu vends, il vend, nous vendons, vous vendez, ils vendent | Standard -re verb: silent 's'/'d' in singular (je vends [vɑ̃], il vend [vɑ̃]). |
| **dépenser** | To spend (money / energy) | `day-pahn-SAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=d%C3%A9penser&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Il dépense trop d'argent pour des gadgets inutiles. *(He spends too much money on useless gadgets.)* | je dépense, tu dépenses, il dépense, nous dépensons, vous dépensez, ils dépensent | Dépenser is for money/energy; passer is for time (passer du temps). |
| **économiser** | To save (money) / To economize | `ay-koh-noh-mee-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%A9conomiser&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | J'économise chaque mois pour partir en voyage. *(I save each month to go on a trip.)* | j'économise, nous économisons | Regular -er verb [ekɔnɔmize]. Single 's' pronounced [z]. |
| **le kilo / le kilogramme** | Kilogram / Kilo | `luh kee-LOH / luh kee-loh-GRAHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20kilo%20/%20le%20kilogramme&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Je voudrais un kilo de tomates bien mûres, s'il vous plaît. *(I would like a kilo of ripe tomatoes, please.)* | les kilos | Quantity requires 'de' without article: un kilo de pommes (NOT *des pommes). |
| **le gramme** | Gram | `luh GRAHM` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20gramme&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Mettez-moi deux cents grammes de fromage râpé. *(Give me two hundred grams of grated cheese.)* | les grammes | Masculine noun [ɡʁam]. Doubles 'mm'. Followed by de: 200 grammes de. |
| **le litre** | Liter | `luh LEET-ruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20litre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Il faut un litre de lait pour préparer les crêpes. *(One liter of milk is needed to make the crepes.)* | les litres | Masculine noun [litʁ]. Followed by de: un litre d'eau. |
| **une livre** | Pound (500 grams in France) / British currency | `oon LEE-vruh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20livre&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Donnez-moi une livre de cerises fraîches. *(Give me a half-kilo / pound of fresh cherries.)* | les livres | Gender trap! Une livre (f.) = pound (500g metric weight or currency £); un livre (m.) = a book! |
| **une douzaine** | A dozen (12 items) | `oon doo-ZEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20douzaine&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'achète une douzaine d'œufs frais de la ferme. *(I buy a dozen fresh farm eggs.)* | les douzaines | Approximate number suffix -aine (une dizaine, une douzaine, une vingtaine). Followed by de: une douzaine d'œufs. |
| **un morceau de** | A piece of / A chunk of | `œ̃ mor-SOH duh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20morceau%20de&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Puis-je avoir un morceau de ce fromage de chèvre ? *(May I have a piece of this goat cheese?)* | des morceaux de (takes -x) | Nouns in -eau take -x in plural [mɔʁso]. Followed by de without article. |
| **une tranche de** | A slice of | `oon TRAHNSH duh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=une%20tranche%20de&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Coupez-moi deux tranches fines de jambon blanc. *(Cut me two thin slices of cooked ham.)* | des tranches de | Feminine noun [tʁɑ̃ʃ]. Tranche de pain, tranche de gâteau. |
| **un paquet de** | A packet of / A pack of / A parcel | `œ̃ pah-KEH duh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=un%20paquet%20de&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | N'oublie pas d'acheter un paquet de café moulu. *(Don't forget to buy a pack of ground coffee.)* | des paquets de | Silent 't' [pakɛ]. Followed by de: un paquet de pâtes. |
| **Ça fait combien ?** | How much does that come to? / What's the total? | `sah FEH kohn-BYA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=%C3%87a%20fait%20combien%20%3F&op=translate) | `Idiomatic Totaling Phrase` | Voici tout. Ça fait combien au total, s'il vous plaît ? *(Here is everything. How much does that come to in total, please?)* | Invariable idiom with faire | Verb faire in 3rd person: ça fait 12 euros (that comes to 12€). |
| **Gardez la monnaie** | Keep the change | `gar-DAY lah moh-NEH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=Gardez%20la%20monnaie&op=translate) | `Imperative Expression` | Merci pour votre service rapide, gardez la monnaie ! *(Thank you for your prompt service, keep the change!)* | Informal: Garde la monnaie | Imperative of garder with vous. Polite phrase when rounding up a cash payment. |

## 26. City Locations and Public Buildings (La ville et les lieux publics)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **la ville** | City / Town | `lah VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20ville&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Lyon est une belle ville historique traversée par deux fleuves. *(Lyon is a beautiful historic city crossed by two rivers.)* | les villes | Double 'll' pronounced [l] here (NOT liquid [j]): [vil]. One of few exceptions with village and mille. |
| **le centre-ville** | City center / Downtown | `luh sahn-truh-VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20centre-ville&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous nous promenons dans les rues piétonnes du centre-ville. *(We are walking through the pedestrian streets of the city center.)* | les centres-villes | Hyphenated noun. Both parts take -s in plural: des centres-villes. |
| **la rue** | Street / Road | `lah REW` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20rue&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La boulangerie se trouve juste en face, dans cette rue. *(The bakery is right across, in this street.)* | les rues | Feminine noun [ʁy]. In France, addresses are written: 12, rue de la Paix. |
| **l'avenue (f.) / le boulevard (m.)** | Avenue / Boulevard | `lahv-NYOO / luh bool-VAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27avenue%20%28f.%29%20/%20le%20boulevard%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine / Masculine (f./m.)` | L'avenue des Champs-Élysées est célèbre dans le monde entier. *(The Avenue des Champs-Élysées is world-famous.)* | les avenues / les boulevards | Avenue is feminine; boulevard is masculine with silent 'd' [bulvaʁ]. |
| **la place** | Town square / Plaza / Seat | `lah PLAHSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20place&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Rendez-vous sur la place du marché à midi. *(Meet on the market square at noon.)* | les places | Dual meaning: town square (la place de la Concorde) and a seat (une place assise). |
| **le quartier** | Neighborhood / District / Quarter | `luh kar-TYAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20quartier&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Montmartre est un quartier très pittoresque et animé. *(Montmartre is a very picturesque and lively neighborhood.)* | les quartiers | Silent 'r' [kaʁtje]. Masculine noun. |
| **la mairie / l'hôtel de ville** | Town hall / City hall | `lah may-REE / loh-tel duh VEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20mairie%20/%20l%27h%C3%B4tel%20de%20ville&op=translate) | `Feminine / Masculine (f./m.)` | Le mariage civil a lieu à la mairie samedi matin. *(The civil marriage takes place at the town hall on Saturday morning.)* | les mairies / les hôtels de ville | False friend: 'l'hôtel de ville' is city hall, NOT a commercial hotel! 'Mairie' for boroughs/small towns. |
| **la poste** | Post office / Postal service | `lah POHST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20poste&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je dois envoyer cette lettre recommandée à la poste. *(I must send this registered letter at the post office.)* | les postes | Feminine for the postal service (La Poste); masculine 'le poste' = job position or TV set! |
| **la banque** | Bank | `lah BAHN-kuh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20banque&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Je vais retirer de l'argent au distributeur de la banque. *(I'm going to withdraw money from the bank ATM.)* | les banques | Feminine noun [bɑ̃k]. ATM is 'un distributeur automatique de billets' (DAB). |
| **la gare** | Railway station / Train station | `lah GAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20gare&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Le train entre en gare voie numéro quatre. *(The train enters the station on track number 4.)* | les gares | Feminine noun [ɡaʁ]. Train station = la gare; bus station = la gare routière. |
| **l'aéroport (m.)** | Airport | `lah-ay-roh-POR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27a%C3%A9roport%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous partons pour l'aéroport trois heures avant le décollage. *(We leave for the airport three hours before takeoff.)* | les aéroports | Silent 't' [aeʁɔpɔʁ]. Masculine noun. |
| **l'hôpital (m.)** | Hospital | `loh-pee-TAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27h%C3%B4pital%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les urgences de l'hôpital sont ouvertes jour et nuit. *(The hospital emergency room is open day and night.)* | Irregular plural: les hôpitaux (irregular -aux) | Nouns in -al take -aux in plural: un hôpital -> des hôpitaux. Silent 'h' [ɔpital]. Circumflex on 'ô'. |
| **l'église (f.)** | Church | `lay-GLEEZ` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%A9glise%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les cloches de la vieille église sonnent à midi. *(The old church bells chime at noon.)* | les églises | Feminine noun [eɡliz]. Liaison: les‿églises [le-z-eɡliz]. Cathedral is 'la cathédrale'. |
| **le musée** | Museum | `luh myoo-ZAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20mus%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le musée du Louvre attire des millions de visiteurs chaque année. *(The Louvre museum attracts millions of visitors every year.)* | les musées | Gender exception! Noun ending in -ée that is MASCULINE (le musée, le lycée, le trophée). |
| **le parc** | Park / Public park | `luh PARK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20parc&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les familles se promènent dans le parc les après-midis ensoleillés. *(Families walk in the park on sunny afternoons.)* | les parcs | Sounded 'c' [paʁk]. Masculine noun. |
| **la bibliothèque** | Public library | `lah bee-blee-oh-TEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20biblioth%C3%A8que&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | J'emprunte des romans policiers à la bibliothèque municipale. *(I borrow detective novels from the municipal library.)* | les bibliothèques | Loans books for free/cheap; do not confuse with 'la librairie' (bookstore where you buy books). |
| **le cinéma** | Cinema / Movie theater | `luh see-nay-MAH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20cin%C3%A9ma&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | On va au cinéma voir le dernier film ce soir ? *(Are we going to the movies to see the latest film tonight?)* | les cinémas | Colloquially called 'le ciné'. Preposition: aller au cinéma (à + le = au). |
| **le théâtre** | Theater (playhouse) | `luh tay-AH-truh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20th%C3%A9%C3%A2tre&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Nous avons réservé des places au théâtre pour voir Molière. *(We booked theater tickets to see Molière.)* | les théâtres | Circumflex on 'â' [teatʁ]. Silent 'h'. Preposition: aller au théâtre. |
| **le stade** | Stadium / Sports arena | `luh STAHD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20stade&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le match de football se déroule au Stade de France. *(The football match takes place at the Stade de France.)* | les stades | Masculine noun [stad]. |
| **la piscine municipale** | Municipal swimming pool | `lah pee-SEEN myoo-nee-see-PAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20piscine%20municipale&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Les enfants prennent des cours de natation à la piscine. *(Children take swimming lessons at the pool.)* | les piscines municipales | 'sc' sounds [s] [pisin]. Swimming cap (bonnet de bain) is mandatory in French pools! |

In rural areas, it is 'la gendarmerie'. |
| **l'office de tourisme (m.)** | Tourist information office | `loh-FEESS duh too-REEZ-muh` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27office%20de%20tourisme%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Demandez un plan de la ville gratuit à l'office de tourisme. *(Ask for a free city map at the tourist office.)* | les offices de tourisme | Masculine noun [ɔfis də tuʁizm]. Liaison: à l'office [a lɔfis]. |
| **l'arrêt de bus (m.)** | Bus stop | `lah-REH duh BOOSS` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27arr%C3%AAt%20de%20bus%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Attendez à l'arrêt de bus jusqu'à l'arrivée de la ligne 12. *(Wait at the bus stop until line 12 arrives.)* | les arrêts de bus | Circumflex on 'ê' in arrêt [aʁɛ]. Sounded 's' in bus [bys]. |
| **la station de métro** | Subway station / Metro stop | `lah stah-SYOHN duh may-TROH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20station%20de%20m%C3%A9tro&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La station de métro la plus proche est à deux cents mètres. *(The nearest metro station is 200 meters away.)* | les stations de métro | Nouns in -tion are feminine [stasjɔ̃]. Subway uses 'station'; bus uses 'arrêt'. |
| **le pont** | Bridge | `luh POHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20pont&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Traversez le pont au-dessus de la rivière pour rejoindre l'autre rive. *(Cross the bridge over the river to reach the other bank.)* | les ponts | Silent 't' [pɔ̃]. Expression: 'faire le pont' = to take a long weekend when a holiday falls on Tuesday/Thursday! |
| **le trottoir** | Sidewalk / Pavement | `luh troh-TWAHR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20trottoir&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les piétons doivent marcher sur le trottoir pour leur sécurité. *(Pedestrians must walk on the sidewalk for safety.)* | les trottoirs | Masculine noun [tʁɔtwaʁ]. Doubles 'tt'. |
| **le feu rouge** | Red traffic light / Traffic signal | `luh FUH ROOZH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20feu%20rouge&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La voiture s'arrête au feu rouge au carrefour. *(The car stops at the red light at the intersection.)* | les feux rouges (takes -x) | Words ending in -eu take -x in plural: les feux [fø]. Traffic light system = les feux tricolores. |
| **le passage piéton** | Pedestrian crossing / Crosswalk | `luh pah-SAHZH pyay-TOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20passage%20pi%C3%A9ton&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Traversez toujours sur le passage piéton quand le bonhomme est vert. *(Always cross on the pedestrian crossing when the figure is green.)* | les passages piétons | Nouns in -age are masculine [pasaʒ]. Nasal piéton [pjetɔ̃]. |
| **le rond-point** | Roundabout / Traffic circle | `luh ROHN-PWA(N)` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20rond-point&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Au rond-point, prenez la deuxième sortie vers le centre. *(At the roundabout, take the second exit towards the center.)* | les ronds-points | France has more roundabouts than any other country in the world! Silent 'd' and 't' [ʁɔ̃pwɛ̃]. |
| **se promener** | To take a walk / To stroll | `suh prohm-NAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=se%20promener&op=translate) | `Reflexive Verb (1st group stem-changing)` | Nous nous promenons souvent au bord du canal le dimanche. *(We often take a walk along the canal on Sunday.)* | je me promène, tu te promènes, il se promène, nous nous promenons, vous vous promenez, ils se promènent | Stem changer: 'e' changes to 'è' before silent endings (je me promène [pʁɔmɛn]). |
