## 35. Cardinal Points and Basic Geography (Les points cardinaux et géographie)

| French (with Article) | English Meaning | Pronunciation | Audio | Gender / Word Type | Example Sentence | Plural / Conjugation | Liaison & Teacher's Notes |
|:---|:---|:---|:---:|:---|:---|:---|:---|
| **le point cardinal** | Cardinal point / Compass point | `luh PWA(N) kar-dee-NAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20point%20cardinal&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Les quatre points cardinaux sont le nord, le sud, l'est et l'ouest. *(The four cardinal points are north, south, east, and west.)* | les points cardinaux (irregular -aux) | Adjective cardinal -> cardinaux takes -aux in plural [kaʁdino]. |
| **le nord** | North | `luh NOR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20nord&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Lille est une grande ville située dans le nord de la France. *(Lille is a large city located in the north of France.)* | Preposition: dans le nord / au nord de | Silent 'd' [nɔʁ]. When capitalized (le Nord), refers to the administrative region or Arctic. |
| **le sud** | South | `luh SOOD` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20sud&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Marseille et Nice bénéficient du climat ensoleillé du sud. *(Marseille and Nice enjoy the sunny climate of the south.)* | Preposition: dans le sud / au sud de | Final 'd' IS sounded: [syd]! Colloquially, southern France is called 'le Midi'. |
| **l'est (m.)** | East | `LEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27est%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Strasbourg se trouve tout à fait à l'est, près du Rhin. *(Strasbourg is located completely in the east, near the Rhine.)* | Preposition: à l'est / dans l'est | Both 's' and 't' ARE sounded: [ɛst]! Do not confuse with 'il est' (he is [ɛ]). |
| **l'ouest (m.)** | West | `LWEST` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27ouest%20%28m.%29&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La Bretagne et la Normandie sont situées dans l'ouest. *(Brittany and Normandy are located in the west.)* | Preposition: à l'ouest / dans l'ouest | Both 's' and 't' ARE sounded: [wɛst]! |
| **la Terre** | The Earth / The globe / Soil | `lah TAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20Terre&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La Terre tourne autour du Soleil en trois cent soixante-cinq jours. *(The Earth revolves around the Sun in 365 days.)* | N/A (proper noun for planet) | Capitalized when referring to the planet (la Terre). Lowercase 'la terre' = soil / earth / land. |
| **le continent** | Continent | `luh kohn-tee-NAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20continent&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | L'Eurasie est la plus vaste masse continentale. *(Eurasia is the largest continental landmass.)* | les continents | Silent 't' [kɔ̃tinɑ̃]. Nouns ending in -ent are masculine. |
| **l'Europe (f.)** | Europe | `luh-ROHP` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Europe%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Continent (f.)` | La France et l'Allemagne se trouvent en Europe. *(France and Germany are located in Europe.)* | Preposition: en Europe | All continents in French are FEMININE and take 'en': en Europe [ɑ̃.n‿øʁɔp] with [n] liaison. |
| **l'Asie (f.)** | Asia | `lah-ZEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Asie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Continent (f.)` | L'Asie est le continent le plus peuplé du monde. *(Asia is the most populous continent in the world.)* | Preposition: en Asie | Single 's' pronounced [z] [azi]. Preposition: en Asie [ɑ̃.n‿azi] with [n] liaison. |
| **l'Afrique (f.)** | Africa | `lah-FREEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Afrique%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Continent (f.)` | De nombreux pays d'Afrique ont le français comme langue officielle. *(Many African countries have French as an official language.)* | Preposition: en Afrique | Feminine continent. Preposition: en Afrique [ɑ̃.n‿afʁik]. |
| **l'Amérique (f.)** | America / The Americas | `lah-may-REEK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Am%C3%A9rique%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Continent (f.)` | L'Amérique du Nord et l'Amérique du Sud forment le continent américain. *(North and South America form the American continent.)* | Preposition: en Amérique | Preposition: en Amérique [ɑ̃.n‿ameʁik]. Feminine. |
| **l'Océanie (f.)** | Oceania / Australasia | `loh-say-ah-NEE` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27Oc%C3%A9anie%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine Continent (f.)` | L'Australie et la Nouvelle-Zélande font partie de l'Océanie. *(Australia and New Zealand are part of Oceania.)* | Preposition: en Océanie | Preposition: en Océanie [ɑ̃.n‿ɔseani]. Feminine. |
| **la frontière** | Border / Frontier | `lah frohn-TYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20fronti%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La France partage une frontière terrestre avec six pays européens. *(France shares a land border with six European countries.)* | les frontières | Feminine noun [fʁɔ̃tjɛʁ]. E.g., frontière franco-espagnole. |
| **la capitale** | Capital city | `lah kah-pee-TAHL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20capitale&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Paris est la capitale politique et culturelle de la France. *(Paris is the political and cultural capital of France.)* | les capitales | Feminine noun [kapital]. As adjective: une lettre capitale (a capital letter). |
| **le fleuve** | River (flowing into the sea/ocean) | `luh FLUHV` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20fleuve&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | La Seine est le fleuve qui traverse la ville de Paris. *(The Seine is the river that flows through Paris.)* | les fleuves | Crucial French geography distinction: 'un fleuve' flows directly into the sea/ocean (la Seine, la Loire, le Rhône); 'une rivière' flows into another river! |
| **la rivière** | River / Tributary (flowing into another river) | `lah ree-VYAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20rivi%C3%A8re&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La Marne est une rivière qui se jette dans la Seine. *(The Marne is a river that flows into the Seine.)* | les rivières | Feminine noun [ʁivjɛʁ]. Contrasts with masculine 'le fleuve'. |
| **le lac** | Lake | `luh LAHK` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20lac&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le lac Léman s'étend entre la France et la Suisse. *(Lake Geneva stretches between France and Switzerland.)* | les lacs | Sounded 'c' in singular [lak]. Silent 'c' in plural les lacs [la] in classical diction, though [lak] is common today. |
| **l'île (f.)** | Island / Isle | `LEEL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=l%27%C3%AEle%20%28f.%29&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La Corse est une magnifique île montagneuse de Méditerranée. *(Corsica is a magnificent mountainous Mediterranean island.)* | les îles | Circumflex on 'î' [il]. Mandatory [z] liaison: les‿îles [le-z-il]. Paris heart: l'île de la Cité. |
| **la péninsule** | Peninsula | `lah pay-na(n)-SYOOL` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20p%C3%A9ninsule&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La péninsule ibérique comprend l'Espagne et le Portugal. *(The Iberian peninsula comprises Spain and Portugal.)* | les péninsules | Feminine noun [penɛ̃syl]. Also: la presqu'île (literally 'almost an island'). |
| **la colline** | Hill / Hillock | `lah koh-LEEN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20colline&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La basilique du Sacré-Cœur domine Paris depuis la colline de Montmartre. *(The Sacré-Cœur basilica overlooks Paris from the hill of Montmartre.)* | les collines | Double 'll' pronounced [l] [kɔlin]. Contrast with la montagne. |
| **la vallée** | Valley | `lah vah-LAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20vall%C3%A9e&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La vallée de la Loire est réputée pour ses châteaux Renaissance. *(The Loire Valley is renowned for its Renaissance castles.)* | les vallées | Feminine noun [vale]. Double 'll' sounds [l]. |
| **la forêt** | Forest / Woods | `lah foh-REH` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20for%C3%AAt&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | Nous aimons ramasser des champignons dans la forêt à l'automne. *(We love picking mushrooms in the forest in autumn.)* | les forêts | Circumflex on 'ê' [fɔʁɛ]. Silent 't'. Feminine noun. |
| **le désert** | Desert | `luh day-ZAIR` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%A9sert&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Le désert du Sahara s'étend sur une immense superficie en Afrique. *(The Sahara desert covers an immense area in Africa.)* | les déserts | Single 's' pronounced [z] [dezɛʁ]! Silent 't'. Compare with le dessert [desɛʁ] (with double ss)! |

Dual meaning: coastline, hill slope, and culinary rib (côte de bœuf). |
| **la région** | Region / Administrative region | `lah ray-ZHYOHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=la%20r%C3%A9gion&op=translate) | `Feminine (f.)` | La France métropolitaine est découpée en treize grandes régions. *(Metropolitan France is divided into 13 major regions.)* | les régions | Feminine noun in -on [ʁeʒjɔ̃]. |
| **le département** | Department (administrative territorial division) | `luh day-par-tuh-MAHN` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=le%20d%C3%A9partement&op=translate) | `Masculine (m.)` | Chaque département français possède un numéro à deux chiffres. *(Each French department has a 2-digit number.)* | les départements | France has 101 départements (e.g., 75 = Paris, 13 = Bouches-du-Rhône). |

| **entourer** | To surround / To encircle | `ahn-too-RAY` | [🔊 Audio](https://translate.google.com/?sl=fr&tl=en&text=entourer&op=translate) | `Verb (1st group -er)` | Des remparts médiévaux entourent la cité historique. *(Medieval ramparts surround the historic city.)* | j'entoure, tu entoures, il entoure, nous entourons, vous entourez, ils entourent | Regular -er verb [ɑ̃tuʁe]. Preposition: entouré de (surrounded by). |
